❯ /effort                                                                       
  ⎿  Set effort level to xhigh (saved as your default for new sessions): Deeper
     reasoning than high, just below maximum (on supported models)

❯ Kivactは度重なる大規模な機能追加によりかなり肥大化してきたし、UIフレームワー  
  クとしても動作が鈍重になってきたように感じる。                                
                                                                                
  そこで、ここで破壊的かつ抜本的で根本的な構造改革とリファクタリング、およびパ  
  フォーマンスチューニングを行うことで、膿を出し切り、現状の機能を保ちつつスリ  
  ムで高速かつ軽量なUIフレームワークに生まれ変わることにする。                  
                                                                                
  「高速かつ軽量なUIフレームワーク」を謳うにあたって、下記を主要KPIとする。     
                                                                                
  【主要KPI】                                                                   
                                                                                
  起動時間（起動の速さ）/ プロセス起動～初回描画・操作可能までの時間（ms）      
  メモリ使用量（軽量さ）/ アイドル時・操作時・ピーク時の使用量（MiB）           
  CPU使用量（省リソース性） / アイドル時のCPU使用率・操作時のCPU処理時間        
  フレーム時間（描画の滑らかさ） / 描画間隔・フレーム処理時間のP95/P99（ms）    
  UI更新レイテンシ（UIエンジンの処理速度） /                                    
  入力や状態変更～描画反映までの時間（ms）                                      
  バイナリサイズ（配布物の軽量さ） /                                            
  Releaseビルドの実行ファイル・依存ライブラリの容量                             
                                                                                
  この主要KPIについて、現時点のKivactに対して、どのくらいの改善目標が現実的か、 
  分析してレポートしてほしい。                                                  
  なお、実装はこのマシンではなく別のマシンで行う可能性が高いので、目標値は倍率  
  表記が良いだろうと思う。                                                      
                                                                                
  ---                                                                           
                                                                                
  リファクタリング、および実装の方針は下記である。下記を重視すること。          
                                                                                
  【実装方針】                                                                  
                                                                                
  - Rust 未経験のReact・SolidJS開発者がスムーズに書ける公開APIになっているか    
  - 公開APIはわかりやすく統一的なAPIであるか                                    
  - 公開APIは普段はJavaScriptやPythonを書いているRust未経験の人でも容易に記述で 
  きるか                                                                        
  - Kivact内部が統一的でシンプルな構造になっているか                            
  - Kivactの長期保守性                                                          
  - docs/for-kivact-developer/strategy.md を簡単に遂行できるか                  
                                                                                
  モジュール分割を行うよりはクレート分割を行うべきだ。ただし、クレート分割は目  
  的ではなく手段であり、境界がはっきりしていないのに分割してはいけない。        
                                                                                
  また、存在する暗黙的な状態マシンは全て解消せよ。                              
                                                                                
  ---                                                                           
                                                                                
  まずは作業ブランチを作成せよ。                                                
  その後、上記の実装方針に従って、リポジトリルートに設計書と、作業指示書を作成  
  せよ。                                                                        
  実装作業は、新たに作成した作業ブランチにて、作業指示書をもとに別のAIにサブエ  
  ージェントを用いて実装してもらう。                                            
  別のAIが別途設計や探索をする必要はなく、ただ単に作業指示書と設計書をもとに淡  
  々と実装できる程度に詳細に記述すること。                                      
  別のAIは賢くない（Haiku 5.5 High程度）。                                      
                                                                                
  実装速度を向上するため、サブエージェントは最大6体を並列稼働させる。サブエージ 
  ェントの割り振りについても指示書に記載せよ。                                  
  また、実装途中に何度も時間のかかるテストを回すことは避けよ。                  

  Searched for 1 pattern, ran 4 shell commands, recalled 8 memories, searched memories (ctrl+o to expand)

› Message from Explore
  **Kivact per-frame pipeline: findings (sections 1–7)**

  To keep the citations short, every path starts at
  `C:\w\kashiwade\kivact\crates\`. So `kivact-engine\src\host\pump.rs:454` means
  `C:\w\kashiwade\kivact\crates\kivact-engine\src\host\pump.rs:454`. On Windows
  the top-level window uses the winit port; `port\win32` is compiled only for
  the `embed` feature (`arvane-native\src\port.rs`).

  ## 1. Frame trigger and idle behaviour
  - **How the loop waits:** after each callback the result sets winit's control
  flow. `ShellControl::Wait` becomes `ControlFlow::Wait`; `WakeAt(ns)` becomes
  `WaitUntil` (`arvane-native\src\port\winit\runtime.rs:199-210`). When the
  deadline passes, `StartCause::ResumeTimeReached` turns into
  `ShellEvent::Wake(CLIENT)` (runtime.rs:426-434).
  - **Wakes from other threads:** they go through the event-loop proxy, with an
  `AtomicU8` that merges wake reasons (runtime.rs:503-508, 352-413). The reasons
  are CLIENT, ASSETS, A11Y_ACTIONS, ENVIRONMENT and GPU_STEP
  (`arvane-native\src\wake.rs:4-10`).
  - **The next deadline** is computed in `Host::control`
  (`kivact-engine\src\host\pump.rs:765-810`). It is the earliest of:
    - "now", if events or effects are queued;
    - the client's `next_wake` (timers);
    - `frame_deadline`, which exists only while frames are requested
  (animations, smooth scroll; `kivact-engine\src\model\frame\ticks.rs:278-295`);
    - the retry of a skipped present (backs off from 1 to 16 display periods;
  `arvane\src\presenter\frame_policy.rs:247-254`);
    - the drawable-export retry.

    With none of these it waits indefinitely.
  - **Who asks for a redraw** (`window.request_redraw()`,
  `arvane-native\src\port\winit\window.rs:277`):
    - `finish_window` at the end of every pump, when a paint is due
  (`kivact-engine\src\host\window_changes.rs:198-201`);
    - `defer` after input (pump.rs:737-742);
    - the arvane driver, when a GPU start step finishes and the GPU can draw
  (`arvane\src\window.rs:447-455`);
    - the OS, through `RedrawRequested`, which becomes `ShellEvent::Redraw`
  (`window_events.rs:202`).
  - **What makes a paint due:** `Model::sync` sets "redraw needed" on a paint
  flag, new fonts, moved geometry, or any node marked written or redrawn
  (`kivact-engine\src\model\interact\sync.rs:159-168`;
  `paint\list\invalidation.rs:92-94`). It is also set on resize, resume, a new
  surface, and focus after a skipped frame (`host\events.rs:131,360,405,413`),
  and when the devtools overlay changes (pump.rs:897).
  - **Input batching:** input is applied to the model at once. The client turn
  runs at the next wake (`WakeAt(now)`), so a burst of input costs one turn, one
  layout and one frame (pump.rs:733-763).
  - **Idle work: none periodic.**
    - There is no cursor blink (no "blink" anywhere in the crates), no polling
  thread on Windows and no frame clock while nothing is requested.
    - The refresh rate is read only when the window is created or moves to
  another monitor (`window_events.rs:401-409`). The locale is read on
  `Focused(true)` (395-400).
    - Every winit event calls `double_click()`, which queries
  `GetDoubleClickTime` and `GetSystemMetrics` (`window_events.rs:21`;
  `arvane-native\src\native_input\windows.rs:599-606`).
    - `about_to_wait` runs a cheap drop check per window (runtime.rs:415-424).
    - The only busy-waits are bounded shutdown waits
  (`arvane-native\src\wait.rs:18-29`).
    - Other background sources are platform- or feature-specific: a settings
  watcher thread on Linux only (runtime.rs:509-510), and async-sleep polling in
  devtools (`kivact-core\src\devtools\updates.rs:85`).

  ## 2. Stages and how finely each is invalidated
  - **Turn:** `Host::pump` (pump.rs:454-521) does, for each window,
  `poll_assets` and `settle`, then drains events and runs `client.turn`, then
  window changes, effects, externals and `settle` again (pump.rs:404-438). At
  most 64 turns (`TURN_LIMIT`).
  - **Redraw:** `settle_window`, then `scroll_inputs`, then `composition()`,
  then `Window::present` (`host\events.rs:248-306`; pump.rs:292-361). The
  accessibility publish runs on the following pump, only once no paint is due
  (window_changes.rs:202-211).
  - **Style resolve:** done on the client side, as one reactive Render effect
  per node. It reruns only when a signal it read changes
  (`kivact-core\src\view\mount\style.rs:96-140`). Engine setters skip writes of
  equal values (`kivact-engine\src\model\apply.rs:203-205`). State styles (hover
  and so on) are merged in the engine each time paint is read
  (`model\states.rs:182-193`).
  - **Sync:** one dispatcher (sync.rs:96-170). It derives `Look` only for nodes
  that were written (150-153).
  - **Layout (Taffy 0.14):**
    - It runs only when a pass is requested (`model\changes.rs:264-269`).
  Remeasures that give the same size are skipped
  (`model\interact\geometry.rs:148-153`), and `compute` returns early when
  nothing changed (`kivact-layout\src\layout.rs:67`).
    - Invalidation clears caches up the ancestor chain. It stops at an
  already-empty cache, an overlay, a fixed box or an absolute box
  (`kivact-layout\src\tree.rs:597-638`).
    - **But each pass still does O(N) work:**
      - the root cache is cleared (tree.rs:837-849);
      - `taffy::round_layout` visits every node (tree.rs:856; taffy-0.14.0
  `src\compute\mod.rs:219-259`);
      - `set_final_layout` compares every node (tree.rs:441-470);
      - `scale_item_styles` scans a whole column (tree.rs:861-913).
    - After the pass: `report_geometry` (geometry.rs:766-828) and
  `report_images` over all images (sync.rs:229-230).
  - **Paint list:** retained and culled. It is rebuilt only when it is stale
  (`paint\list\walk.rs:410-460`). Things that make it stale ("restack"):
    - any layout computation or scroll (sync.rs:158,166; changes.rs:272-284);
    - a redraw of a node that has state styles, i.e. hover (sync.rs:163-166);
    - every tick while a state motion is active (ticks.rs:159);
    - a write that changes a node's place or stacking (apply.rs:238,313).

    A rebuild calls `layer_facts`, which walks every drawn node
  (`kivact-engine\src\layer.rs:209-255`). The walk itself is proportional to
  what is visible, using a child index for containers with 32+ children
  (walk.rs:28,345-398).
  - **DrawList / composition ("lower"):** rebuilt from scratch every frame. A
  new `SceneOut` is created and every list entry is lowered again
  (`paint\frame_scene.rs:95-137`). Nothing is diffed at the engine level. Canvas
  scenes are cached in a `SceneCache` of at most 128 entries with a linear
  search (`lower\scene_cache.rs:27-59`).
  - **A11y:** runs only after assistive technology activates it
  (events.rs:522-531; `a11y\publish.rs:21-28`).
    - It is incremental in principle: dirt levels (`a11y\state.rs:23-39`), a
  fingerprint diff before sending (publish.rs:42-52), and only changed nodes
  handed to AccessKit (`arvane-native\src\a11y\native.rs:161-184`).
    - However, any geometry change or scroll raises the dirt to "Metadata"
  (geometry.rs:174-176,660). That runs `build_identity_metadata` over all nodes
  (`a11y\build\dirty.rs:144-204`; `a11y\build\geometry.rs:10-18`;
  `a11y\build\node.rs:417-425`) and `build_dirty_nodes`, which walks the whole
  tree (dirty.rs:258-295).
    - An environment change forces a full rebuild (ticks.rs:110).
  - **Layout lint (`src\check\lint`):** compiled only with `debug_assertions`
  (`kivact-engine\src\check.rs:62-63`; sync.rs:213-225). It does not run in
  release.
  - **Compose plan (arvane-compose), every frame:**
    - validate the whole composition (`plan\builder.rs:25`);
    - for each chunk, compute bounds and an FNV hash of every command
  (`plan\damage.rs:141-154`; `plan\hash.rs:561-563`);
    - diff against last frame's index (damage.rs:76-126,157-184). Records are
  kept for 2 frames (`cache.rs:87-126`).

    A chunk is the run of commands of one compositor layer
  (`kivact-engine\src\lower\scene_out.rs:116-160`). An app with no layers has
  one chunk for the whole window.
  - **Raster:**
    - **CPU:** only the changed rectangles are rasterized
  (`cpu\compositor.rs:268-304`). But each rectangle validates and walks the
  chunk's full command list again, with no per-command culling
  (`arvane-render\src\cpu.rs:118-130`;
  `arvane-render\src\draw\painter.rs:91-117`).
    - Damage becomes full-window at 17 or more rectangles, or above 50% of the
  area (damage.rs:22,248-252).
  - **Present:**
    - **CPU:** softbuffer `present_with_damage` when the buffer age is 1.
  Otherwise every pixel is converted (`arvane-native\src\present.rs:69-96`;
  frame_policy.rs:464-482).
    - **GPU:** chunk textures are patched only where damaged
  (`gpu\exec.rs:291-344`). But the output is cleared and all draw ops are
  recomposited every frame (exec.rs:186), then the full window is copied to the
  swapchain (`gpu\surface.rs:132-179`). There is no damage on the GPU present.
  - **Is partial redraw end-to-end?**
    - **CPU:** yes, from rasterization to present.
    - **GPU:** partial chunk raster only; composite and present are full-window.
    - **Engine side:** always a full rebuild of the composition for everything
  visible.

  ## 3. Text
  - **Caches in arvane-text** (`arvane-text\src\cache.rs:300-331`;
  `layouts.rs:26-202`):

    | Cache | Size per generation | Key |
    |---|---|---|
    | shaped | 1024 | `TextKey` |
    | measured | 4096 | `TextKey` |
    | paragraphs (parley `Layout`) | 256 | `layouts::Key` |

    Each cache keeps two generations.
  - **`TextKey` fields** (cache.rs:140-198): owned text `String`, font size
  bits, weight, color (draw only), line height, wrap, align, family, italic,
  variations, font width, optical sizing, max width bits, carets, and an
  optional boxed rich part.
  - **Parley layout reuse:** one parley layout per text and style is reused at
  every width; on a cache miss its lines are broken again for the new width
  (`text_engine.rs:455-478`).
  - **Per frame:** every listed text node does a `shape_glyphs` lookup
  (`kivact-engine\src\paint\text.rs:86-123`). Color is stripped from plain text
  so a hover hits the cache (102-106).
    - Each lookup allocates: `text.to_owned()`, a family clone and a variations
  `Vec` (cache.rs:184-193), plus a `ComputedTextStyle` clone and family
  resolution (`kivact-engine\src\lower\assets.rs:324-331`).
    - The maps use std `HashMap`. A hit returns an `Arc` clone
  (`kivact-types\src\render\shaped.rs:164`).
  - **Reshaping** happens only on a cache miss. More than about 1024 distinct
  visible texts would thrash the generations.
  - **Font registration** drops all caches (sync.rs:127-149;
  text_engine.rs:140-149).
  - **Leaf measurements** are also recorded per node, up to 16 per leaf
  (`kivact-layout\src\tree.rs:108-122`).

  ## 4. Per-node data
  - **Node store:** `NodeStore` has `by_id: IdMap`, `entries:
  Vec<Option<(NodeId, HostNode)>>` and `links: Vec<NodeLinks>`
  (`kivact-engine\src\tree\store.rs:36-42`).
  - **`HostNode`:** 9 fields, including `scenes: [Option<Arc<DrawList>>; 2]` and
  three `Sent<>` report fields (`tree\node.rs:21-38`).
  - **`NodeProperties`:** 11 fields, 7 of them `Option<Box<…>>`, plus
  `NodeInput` with 10 `Option` fields
  (`kivact-engine\src\contract\node.rs:35-59,99-122`). Text nodes hold
  `Option<String>` and `Option<Vec<TextSpan>>`.
  - **Side columns, one entry per slot:**
    - paint: `ink` and `look` (`Look` has about 22 fields;
  `paint\state.rs:53-68`; `layer.rs:51-104`);
    - measure: `natural`, `extents` (boxed), `computed_text` (`Rc`)
  (`measure\state.rs:14-18`);
    - `editors: Vec<Option<Box<EditorNode>>>` (`model\aggregate.rs:31`);
    - layout: about 9 `Vec` columns (`NodeStyle` has about 30 fields), with
  taffy caches and up to 16 recorded leaf measurements
  (`kivact-layout\src\columns.rs:101-161`).
  - **Whole-tree structures:** `NodeTree` keeps several `BTreeSet`s and a
  flat-children cache, `RefCell<BTreeMap<NodeId, Rc<[NodeId]>>>`, which is
  cleared entirely on every node install (`tree\node_tree.rs:13-29,320`).
  `Changes` is a `HashMap<NodeId, Dirty>` (changes.rs:129-142).
  - **Allocations per frame:**
    - a new `SceneOut` / `Composition` (frame_scene.rs:102);
    - a `PaintProps` clone and sanitize per listed node
  (`model\interact\visual.rs:16-20`; states.rs:183-188), again in close for
  nodes with a draw-over scene (frame_scene.rs:362);
    - a `step.opened()` `Vec` at each open and close (paint\state.rs:205-228;
  frame_scene.rs:186,367);
    - an ancestor `Vec` from `tree.chain` in `text_color`, called twice per text
  node (states.rs:228-252; paint\text.rs:95,141);
    - a clone of the devtools overlay (pump.rs:314);
    - in the plan: a hash `Vec` and `Arc` index per chunk, and several
  `HashSet`s, with a possible re-plan loop (damage.rs:142-154;
  builder.rs:27-42,103-129);
    - CPU: a new `Pixmap` per fully redrawn chunk, and a full pixmap clone per
  filter op (compositor.rs:299,365);
    - GPU: a new texture and a separate submit per damaged rectangle, and a bind
  group per present (exec.rs:315-336; surface.rs:146);
    - a `Vec<ShellEvent>` per winit event (window_events.rs:23);
    - in release builds, a `String` per host event from `describe(event)`,
  cloned again (`kivact-core\src\launch\runtime.rs:277,452`;
  `kivact-runtime\src\reactive\diag.rs:220`);
    - on each paint-list rebuild: one `Vec` per node from `flat_children`
  (geometry.rs:83-85).
  - **Tracing:** no per-node spans; no `span!` or `#[instrument]` in the engine
  or arvane.

  ## 5. O(N total nodes) work even when one node changes
  - **Every frame:**
    - lowering every visible list entry (frame_scene.rs:106-135);
    - plan validation, bounds and hashing of every command (builder.rs:25;
  damage.rs:141-154);
    - each damaged CPU rectangle walking the whole chunk (painter.rs:115-117;
  cpu.rs:118);
    - the full GPU composite and blit;
    - the full pixel conversion in the CPU fallback path
  (frame_policy.rs:475-479).
  - **Every layout pass:** `round_layout`, `set_final_layout` comparisons,
  `scale_item_styles`, and `report_images`.
  - **Every paint-list rebuild** (after a layout, a scroll, a hover on a node
  with state styles, or every animation tick): `layer_facts`, plus a `drawn()`
  ancestor walk per node, so roughly O(N·depth) (layer.rs:209-255;
  node_tree.rs:241-243,303-316).
  - **Every a11y publish at Metadata level** (while assistive technology is
  active): two whole-tree walks and `BTreeMap` inserts.
  - **Font registration:** every text node is remeasured.
  - **Any node install:** the flat-children cache is reset.

  ## 6. GPU vs CPU
  - **Default renderer on Windows:** GPU.
    - The `kivact` crate's default features are `["gpu"]`
  (`kivact\Cargo.toml:22`), and `prefer_gpu` defaults to true
  (`arvane\src\window.rs:278-284`).
    - `ARVANE_RENDERER` overrides the choice (`arvane\src\env.rs:38-43`).
    - Windows tries Vulkan, then DX12
  (`arvane-compose\src\gpu\context.rs:229-237`).
    - The GPU rasterizer is vello_hybrid; the CPU one is vello_cpu.
  - **GPU init is asynchronous.**
    - The device is created on an `arvane-gpu-init` thread, which calls
  `pollster::block_on` on adapter and device requests
  (`arvane\src\presenter\gpu.rs:346-358,604-609`; context.rs:167-197).
    - The first frames are drawn on the CPU through softbuffer
  (window.rs:263-266,421-427).
    - When a GPU_STEP wake arrives, the UI thread creates the wgpu surface
  synchronously (frame_policy.rs:202-211,559-578). The swapchain is configured
  on an `arvane-gpu-configure` thread (gpu.rs:613-631).
    - While configuring, frames are drawn but not shown, and the frame is
  retried (frame_policy.rs:363-371).
    - Once ready, a redraw is requested (window.rs:447-455).
  - **First frame:** it does not wait for the adapter or device.
  - **Fallback to CPU:** on device failure, repeated surface loss, or a frame
  beyond GPU limits (frame_policy.rs:389-416; gpu.rs:470-512).

  ## 7. Debug-only checks that run per frame or per pass
  - **Layout lint after every layout pass that computed** (sync.rs:213-225;
  `check\lint\changed_texts.rs:39-88`). When boxes may have moved it collects
  all text nodes, does an ancestor walk for each, and reshapes those whose box
  changed.
  - **Text-input refresh verification**
  (`kivact-engine\src\text_input\refresh.rs:52-59`).
  - **Structural `debug_assert`s:** an `is_in_subtree` walk on each insert
  (`host\write\writer.rs:174-176`), and checks in child ordering
  (`tree\child_order.rs:97-98`).
  - **Trace hooks:** no-ops unless `KIVACT_TRACE` is set
  (`kivact-core\src\client\trace.rs:39-49,93-96`).
  - **Paint-list self-check and the a11y reference tree:** only with
  `debug_assertions` plus the `testing` feature (`paint\list.rs:91-92`;
  dirty.rs:23).
  - **Dev profile:** the engine, arvane and taffy are built at `opt-level = 3`
  in dev, but `debug_assertions` stays on, so all of the above still runs (root
  `Cargo.toml:137-191`).

  ## 2. Core vocabulary

  **Signals.**
  - `signal(v) -> Signal<T>` (`setup.rs:46`) has `get`, `get_untracked`, `with`,
  `set`, `update`, `project`, `optional` (`handles.rs:55-179`).
  - `memo(f) -> Value<T>` (`setup.rs:116`); there is no `Memo` type.
  - `effect(impl Fn())` (`setup.rs:132`) has no result type, so no `?`. Also
  `effect_on(source, f)`.
  - `var(v) -> Var<T>`: unwatched, and its `get` is never tracked
  (`handles.rs:361-368`).
  - `binding(get, set)` (`setup.rs:73`) and `callback(f) -> Callback<E>`
  (`setup.rs:103`).

  **Copy handles.** All handles are `Copy`; the docs say "You never write
  `.clone()` on a handle" (`docs/.../coming-from-react-or-solid.md:420-422`).
  The five `examples/kivact` apps contain zero `.clone()` calls.

  **Lists.**
  - `for_each(items, |t| key, |row| view)` (`flow/list.rs:118`). A
  `Signal<Vec<T>>` source gives a `Binding<T>` row; a `Value` source gives a
  `Value<T>` row (`list.rs:398-417`).
  - Also `for_index` and `virtual_list`. `for_each` returns `View`, so it gets
  wrapped as `column(for_each(..))` (`examples/kivact/list/src/main.rs:41`).

  **Conditionals.**
  - `show(when, factory).fallback(..)` (`branch.rs:168`; tutorial `05:78-79`).
  - `show_some(opt, |v: Value<T>| ..)` (`branch.rs:345`). The form example
  passes the `text` function itself: `show_some(email_error, text)`
  (`form/src/main.rs:21`).
  - `dynamic(source, |v| match ..)` (`branch.rs:545`) and `error_boundary`.

  **Async.**
  - `resource(source, |s| async move {..})` returns `Result<T, UiError>`
  (`task/resource.rs:44`). It is rendered with `async_view(user, text)`
  (`async/src/main.rs:26`) or `dynamic(move || r.state(), ..)`.
  - Also `action(ActionMode, f)`, `spawn`, `blocking`, and
  `kivact::task::sleep`.

  **Context.** `provide(value, || child)` (`view/boundary.rs:71`).
  `use_context::<T>()` returns `Value<T>` and panics if nothing was provided
  (`setup.rs:188`). `try_use_context` exists but is not in the prelude.

  **Refs and lifecycle.** `node_ref()` with `.node_ref(r)` and `r.focus()`;
  `on_mount` and `on_cleanup` (`setup.rs:160,175`); `component(move || ..)` as a
  setup and owner boundary (`boundary.rs:50`).

  **Styling.**
  - `.style(style().padding(24).gap(12))`, and `.style(move || ..)` for reactive
  styles.
  - Plain integers are logical pixels: `Length` has `From<i32/u32/f32/f64>`
  (`kivact-style/src/length.rs:68-86`).
  - Other units and colors: `pct(100)`, `em()`, `rgb(0x336699)`,
  `ColorToken::*`, `border(1, ColorToken::Border)` (`sheet.rs:752`).

  **Events.**
  - `on_press(impl Into<Callback<()>>)` (`element_data.rs:467`).
  - `on_key`, `on_pointer`, `on_wheel` take `Fn(Event) -> R where R:
  HandlerResult` (`element_data.rs:418-461`). `R` can be `()`, `Handled`,
  `Result<(), UiError>` or `Result<Handled, UiError>` (`handler.rs:70-97`).
  - Most kivact-ui controls report changes through `on_edit` (14 occurrences).

  ## 3. Friction points (all quoted from the source)

  **Occurrence counts.**
  - Kit catalogs (`examples/kivact-ui-*`, about 29.5k lines):

    | Pattern | Count |
    |---|---|
    | `.clone()` | 76 |
    | `.into()` | 355 |
    | `.into_view()` | 89 |
    | `::<` | 185 (98 are `collect::<Vec<_>>`) |
    | `as f64` | 38 |
    | `Some(` | 349 |
    | `.to_owned()` | 211 |

  - `examples/kivact` (379 lines): 0 `.clone()`, 0 `.into()`, 1 turbofish.

  **Snippets.**
  1. Generic children plus a non-prelude path,

  4. Projection closures, `list/src/main.rs:46`: `todo.project(|t| &t.done, |t|

  6. `ButtonVariant` is not in the prelude, `getting-started/06...md:41`:

  7. Type erasure in match arms, `07-async.md:130-140`: three arms each ending

  **Compiler errors the docs prepare readers for.**

  **Custom diagnostic messages in the code.** `IntoValue`, `IntoText`,

  `element_data.rs:509-512`).

  ## 4. Inconsistencies

  - **One name, two meanings.** The `component()` function returns `View`
  (`boundary.rs:50`). `View` does not implement `NodeBuilder`; only `Node`,
  `Scroll`, `Viewport`, `Overlay` and `Primitive` do. So `component(..)` does
  not satisfy the `Component` trait, and the docs have to explain this
  (`02...md:76-77`; `custom-components.md:139-141`).
  - **Children typed four ways.**
    - `V: IntoView` in the concept map (`coming-from...md:73`)
    - `impl IntoViewFn<V>` (`component/main.rs:11`)
    - `body: impl IntoView` (`custom-components.md:157`)
    - `impl IntoChildren` (`:215`)
  - **Callback parameter types differ across kivact-ui.**
    - `on_press` takes `Into<Callback<()>>` (`element_data.rs:467`), but
  `item_row.on_press` takes `impl Fn() -> R` (`item_row.rs:104`).
    - `on_activate` uses `HandlerResult` on menu, tree and command_palette
  (`menu.rs:78`), but plain `impl Fn(K) + 'static` on breadcrumb
  (`breadcrumb.rs:52`).
    - `on_open_change` uses `HandlerResult` in `hover_card.rs:112` but not in
  `navigation_menu.rs:48`; `stepper.rs:153` (`on_step_press`) also lacks it.
    - This contradicts "Wherever a closure returning `()` is accepted, a closure
  returning `Result<(), UiError>` is accepted too"
  (`coming-from...md:177-179`).
    - `on_submit` is `Into<Callback<()>>` on text_input (`text_input.rs:181`)
  but `Fn(String) -> R` on chat_composer (`chat_composer.rs:55`).
  - **Selection state, five shapes.**
    - `select(label, options, selected: IntoBinding<Option<K>>)`
  (`select/handle.rs:58-61`)
    - `radio_group(.., value: IntoBinding<Option<K>>)` (`radio_group.rs:68`)
    - `tabs(.., binding: IntoBinding<K>)` (`tabs.rs:52`)
    - `combobox` and `toggle_group` take a positional `impl IntoSelection<K>`
  (`combobox/handle.rs:29`, `toggle_group.rs:39`)
    - `list(label, items)` takes a `.selection(..)` setter (`list.rs:84`)
    - The item collection is called `options`, `items`, `entries` (`menu.rs:63`)
  or `nodes` (`tree.rs:38`).
  - **Open/visible.**
    - Positional `open` on `dialog` (`dialog.rs:83`), `disclosure`, `window`
  (`window_view.rs:24`) and soft `bottom_sheet` (`bottom_sheet.rs:131`).
    - A `.open(binding)` setter on combobox, hover_card, selection_field and
  alert.
    - `alert` also has `.visible(binding)` (`alert.rs:131,158`).
    - Soft kit: `media_overlay.open(IntoValue<bool>)` is read-only (`:85`);
  `lightbox.open(self, index)` is a command (`:138`); `mobile_nav` has both
  `open(self)` as a command and `.open(binding)` as a setter (`:155,218`).
  - **First argument and its name.** It is called `label` (button, checkbox),
  `title` (dialog, disclosure, alert, item_row) or `value` (`heading.rs:16`).
  Whether it is drawn also varies: it is not drawn for `text_input`, `select`,
  `slider` or `tabs` (`kivact-ui/src/lib.rs:8-34`). The form example therefore
  shows no visible labels.
  - **Setter forms.**
    - Range: `slider.range(..)` setter (`slider.rs:121`) vs `number_input`'s
  positional range (`number_input.rs:126`).
    - Booleans: `tooltip.enabled` (`tooltip.rs:64`) vs `NodeBuilder::disabled`;
  `hide_label` vs `hide_title` (`dialog.rs:193`).
    - Non-reactive setters: `menu.offset(impl Into<f64>)` and `slider.step(impl
  Into<f64>)`.
  - **Kits diverge despite the stated rule** "a kit's methods of the same roles
  take the same names" (`kivact-ui/src/lib.rs:80-81`).
    - Game `icon_menu.on_select` (`kivact-ui-game/src/overlays.rs:40`) and
  creative `SplitButton.on_select` (`kivact-ui-creative/src/feedback.rs:122`) vs
  `menu.on_activate`.
    - Creative also uses `on_pick` (`picker.rs:246`) and `on_item_activate`
  (`outliner/view.rs:130`).
    - Soft kit: `nav_heading.size(ControlSize)` is constant (`:106`);
  `bottom_sheet.height(SheetHeight)` is constant (`:175`);
  `mobile_nav.width(IntoValue<f64>)` vs `layout.width(IntoValue<Length>)`
  (`:223` vs `layout.rs:342`).
  - **Hook naming.** Interaction says constructors are `use_*`
  (`kivact-interaction/src/lib.rs:12`), but `toaster()` and `announcer()` are
  not (`lib.rs:72,139`), and `toaster` is in the prelude.
  - **Handles.** `Binding` has no `update` method (`handles.rs:528-633`), so the
  guide writes `open.set(!open.get())` (`custom-components.md:196`).
  - **Several ways to do the same thing.**
    - Async UI: `async_view` vs `dynamic` plus `match`.
    - Field binding: `bind!(task.done)` (`05...md:29`) vs `.project(..)`
  (`list/main.rs:46`).
    - Row id: `todo.get().id` (`list/main.rs:45`) vs `task.get_untracked().id`
  (`05...md:28`).
    - Text closures with or without `.to_owned()`.
  - **Docs that disagree with the code.**
    - The guide says `provide_*` for themes and locale live in `kivact::view`
  (`coming-from...md:366-367`). They actually live in `kivact::style`
  (`style.rs:15`) and `kivact::i18n` (`i18n.rs:7-9`); `kivact::view` only has
  `provide_direction` (`view.rs:65`).
    - The catalog says dialog content is given to `show`
  (`kivact-ui-basic/src/docs/dialog.rs:20`), but the code uses
  `.content`/`.footer` (`:54-56`).
    - `NodeBuilder::node_mut` is `#[doc(hidden)]` (`element_data.rs:199`), yet
  the guide tells authors to implement it (`custom-components.md:359-363`).
    - The guides import names already in the prelude
  (`custom-components.md:73,184,305,307`), while every example sets
  `#![warn(redundant_imports)]`.

  ## 5. Authoring a component with props

  **Function form: no props struct.** In `component/src/main.rs:8-25` the whole
  component is 18 lines:
  - Props are positional arguments typed `impl IntoText`, `impl
  Into<Callback<()>>` and `impl kivact::view::IntoViewFn<V>`, with a generic
  `V`.
  - It returns `impl Component`.
  - Callers pass closures positionally (`:32-36`).
  - `card` creates `signal(true)` without a `component()` boundary, so the state
  belongs to the caller's `show` branch (`:31`). The docs say to use
  `component(move || ..)` for a view that owns its own state (`02...md:52-57`),
  but then it must return `impl IntoView` and loses `.style()`.

  **Extra rules for reactive props.**
  - Convert once with `.into_value()` or `.into_text_value()`
  (`coming-from...md:122-124`).
  - A custom enum prop needs `constant_value!` (`custom-components.md:30`).
  - A callback used twice needs `.into()` (`coming-from...md:133-135`).

  **Optional props mean a hand-written builder.** The `Notice` example is about
  78 lines (`custom-components.md:319-396`):
  - a struct holding `root: Node`, `Value`s and `Option<Callback>`
  - a `#[track_caller]` constructor
  - `mut self -> Self` setters
  - `impl NodeBuilder { fn node_mut }`
  - `impl IntoView` wrapping `component(move || { let Notice{..} = self;
  root.base_style(..)...; root.into_view() })`

  There are no named or optional arguments and no derive or macro for props. The
  React version would be `function Card({title, onClose, children})` with
  default values in the destructuring.

› Message from Explore
  # State-fields census: kivact (read-only)

  Census is done and no files were modified. Part 1 lists the 1,034 markers by
  count, not one by one: listing all of them would blow the word budget. Instead
  it gives each crate's struct groups and the markers whose reasons are weak.
  Part 2 is the misses, split into two tiers. Items I read in code are marked
  verified. Items I inferred from field docs and names are marked unverified.

  ## How the lint works
  (`dev/xtask/src/state_fields/{check,classify,locals}.rs`,
  `policy/workspace.toml [state_fields]`)

  **Counts as stored state:**
  - `bool`
  - `Option<T>`, except `Option<Callback|Handler|ViewFn|Rc/Box<dyn Fn>>`
  - `Atomic*`
  - `Cell`, `RefCell`, `Signal`, `Var`, `Mutex` or `RwLock` wrapping `bool`,
  `Option` or an integer
  - Integers named exactly `generation|epoch|serial|seq|token|next`, or matching
  `next_*|*_count|pending_*`

  **Counts as phase:** `MachineHandle`, or a workspace enum whose name ends in
  `State` or `Phase`.

  **Fires when:** a struct, or a named-field enum variant, has at least 2 stored
  fields, or a phase plus at least 1 stored field. Statics fire at 2 or more
  per module.

  **What it skips:**
  - **Named helpers (always ignored):** types `Value`, `Binding`, `Generation`,
  `Sent`, `ReportOnce`, `ReportOnceSet`, `Mailbox`, `LocalMailbox`, `Registry`.
  - **Name-based exemptions:** structs ending in
  `Api|Options|Event|Phase|Reason|Input|Report|Tokens|Recipes`, and the listed
  `record_types`. In these only atomics and Cell-wrapped fields count.
  - **Value modules, skipped entirely:** `kivact-engine::contract`,
  `kivact-types::*`, `kivact-devtools-protocol`.
  - **Builders:** any `NodeBuilder` implementation.
  - **Not classified at all:**
    - `OnceCell` / `OnceLock`
    - `Instant`
    - `Vec`, `BTreeSet`, `HashSet` or `VecDeque` used as pending or dirty sets
    - Integers named `revision`, `frame`, `version`, `last_*`, or of type
  `Epoch` / `Revision`
    - Enums not named `*State` / `*Phase` (`Connection`, `ScriptRun`,
  `LastPresent`, `Display`)
  - **Structural gaps:** tuple variants, items not at module top level, and
  anything split across two structs. Function-local `Cell` or `signal(None)`
  state is only listed with `--report`.
  - **Markers:** an `// xtask-allow(state-fields): reason` line on a field takes
  that field out of the count. Struct-level markers and unused markers are
  errors.

  ## Part 2 – What the lint misses (no marker; slips through the gaps above)

  Tier labels:
  - **High:** clear invalid combinations or duplicated sources of truth. Fix
  these first.
  - **Medium:** probable phase, worth a look.
  - **Low:** late-init or latch patterns. Several of these may be acceptable as
  they are.

  ### kivact-runtime

  | Location | Struct | Fields | Invalid combos / why | Enum |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | reactive/data.rs:77 (verified; low) | `Runtime` | `ext:
  OnceCell<Extensions>` | Late-init slot; `OnceCell` is not classified | Pass in

  | task/state.rs:95 (verified; low) | `TaskCell` | `task: OnceCell<RawTask>` |
  Filled after spawn | `Rc::new_cyclic`, or `Slot { Spawning, Spawned(RawTask)
  }` |
  | reactive/data.rs:79-81 (verified; medium) | `Runtime` | `mount_revision`,
  `unstamped: Vec`, `awaiting: VecDeque<(Revision, Mount)>` | Mount lifecycle is
  encoded by which queue it sits in plus a revision comparison | One queue of
  `Mount{stage: Unstamped / Awaiting(Revision)}` |
  | reactive/data.rs:83-84 (low) | `Runtime` | `state: TurnState` × `exit:
  ExitState` | `Exited` + `WakeSent` / `Running` are meaningless | Nest:
  `Lifecycle { Live(TurnState), Draining{deadline, turn}, Exited }` |

  Cell<Generation>` | Generation used as a cancellation flag ("a worker whose id

  ## Suggested lint changes (closing the gaps that produced Part 2)

  1. Classify `OnceCell` / `OnceLock` as stored.
  2. Count `Generation` / `Sent` / `Revision` / `Epoch` as stored when they sit
  next to another stored field.
  3. Treat any workspace enum field as phase-like, not only names ending in
  `State` / `Phase`. This catches `Connection`, `ScriptRun`, `LastPresent` and
  `Display`.
  4. Don't apply role-suffix exemptions to structs holding `Cell`, `RefCell` or
  `Signal` (`NativeInput`).
  5. Stop skipping `kivact-engine::contract` for structs with a bool plus an
  `Option`.
  6. Flag `Vec` / `BTreeSet` fields named `pending*` / `dirty*` / `awaiting*` /
  `relayout*`.
  7. Run the locals check in CI for `signal(None)` / `signal(false)` next to an
  effect that writes another signal.

› Message from Explore
  Citations are relative to `C:\w\kashiwade\kivact\crates\` unless they start
  with `REG\`, which means
  `C:\Users\0300093866\.cargo\registry\src\index.crates.io-1949cf8c6b5b557f\`.
  Platform is Windows, default feature `gpu`. Nothing was built or timed. Cost
  labels are inferred from the code, except the font-folder size, which I
  measured.

  ## Part A — Startup timeline (all on the main/UI thread unless a worker is
  named)

  0. **`App::new`**: `AppOptions::new` builds `Theme::default()` →
  `Theme::light()`, a plain struct literal
  (`kivact-core/src/launch/settings.rs:44`,
  `kivact-style/src/theme/value.rs:396,479`). Kit `theme(mode)` is also plain
  construction (`kivact-ui-basic/src/theme.rs:14`). Eager and cheap.
  1. **`App::run`**: checks sizes, then `Launch::new` stores the root closure
  only, then `run_native` (`kivact-core/src/app.rs:132-141`,
  `platform/window_loop.rs:8`).
  2. **`kivact_engine::run_native`** (`kivact-engine/src/host/launch.rs:25`):
     - `FrameLog::from_env` reads an env var (`:29`, `frame_log.rs:57`).
     - winit `EventLoop::with_user_event().build()`
  (`arvane-native/src/port/winit/runtime.rs:95`).
     - **`arvane::Engine::new()` (`launch.rs:46`) eagerly scans system fonts.**
  `TextEngine::new` uses `FontContext::default()`
  (`arvane-text/src/text_engine.rs:20,32`). fontique's default has
  `system_fonts: true` and runs `System::new`
  (`REG\fontique-0.11.1\src\collection\mod.rs:56-59,252`). That creates a DWrite
  factory, calls `GetSystemFontCollection` and walks every family's names
  (`backend\dwrite.rs:53-66,144-146`). This is blocking and happens before the
  event loop runs; likely more than 5 ms on large font sets.
  3. **`Shell::run`**: `driver()` reads env vars through `Env::read`
  (`arvane/src/window.rs:319,374`, `env.rs:37`). Cheap.
  4. **`resumed` → `start_loop` → `create_window`** (`runtime.rs:468-470`,
  `open.rs:21`):
     - The window is created hidden: `with_visible(false)` (`open.rs:29`), then
  placed and centred.
     - AccessKit adapter is created eagerly (`open.rs:63`,
  `a11y/native.rs:103-117`), but on Windows it is a `SubclassingAdapter` and the
  tree is only built when a screen reader activates it
  (`REG\accesskit_winit-0.33.1\src\platform_impl\windows.rs:38`,
  `native.rs:31-40`). On Linux, accesskit_unix spawns one thread running an
  async executor plus a D-Bus session connection
  (`REG\accesskit_unix-0.22.1\src\context.rs:55-61`).
     - Cheap OS queries: reduced motion, pointer settings, `sys-locale`
  (`open.rs:66-70`, `native_input/locale.rs:14`), subclass install (`:78`),
  monitor refresh rate (`:92`, `window.rs:466-470`). Dark mode is read through
  winit `theme()` (`window.rs:307`), but the frame theme starts as Light
  (`kivact-engine/src/host/launch.rs:65`, `events.rs:15`).
  5. **`Driver::start`** (`arvane/src/window.rs:416-436`): `Presenter::new` (CPU
  compositor), then `start_gpu` spawns the thread **`arvane-gpu-init`**
  (`presenter/gpu.rs:604-608`). That worker runs alongside the UI thread and
  does `GpuContext::new` (`arvane-compose/src/gpu/context.rs:32-118`):
     - Vulkan first, then DX12 on Windows (`:228-234`); wgpu instance, adapter
  and device via `pollster::block_on` (`:157-176,192-197`).
     - `GpuRaster::new` → vello_hybrid `Renderer::new_with`: **5 WGSL modules
  and 8 render pipelines** (`REG\vello_hybrid-0.2.0\src\render\wgpu\mod.rs:1168,
  1173,1245,1252,1259,1266,1311,1386,1400,1494,1498,1571,1577`), plus a 16×16
  warm-up raster (`arvane-render/src/gpu/raster.rs:52-95`).
     - `Pipelines::new`: **5 WGSL modules and 18 pipelines** (12 draw, plus
  graph, filter, mask_step, apply_acc, apply_alpha, clear)
  (`arvane-compose/src/gpu/pipelines.rs:404-470`). The present pipeline is made
  lazily per format (`:475`).
     - `Shaders::new` creates only a layout and a sampler; user-shader pipelines
  are lazy (`shader_cache.rs:28-49`).
     - **Total: about 10 runtime naga WGSL parses and 26 pipeline compiles**,
  with no wgpu `PipelineCache` (`cache: None`, `pipelines.rs:187`,
  `shader_cache.rs:192`). Instance flags are empty (`state.rs:44`). Clearly more
  than 5 ms, but off the UI thread. The module doc says configuring the
  swapchain takes about 0.2 s the first time on some drivers (`gpu.rs:6`).
  6. **Handler start** (`kivact-engine/src/host/launch.rs:116-160`), still
  inside `resumed`, window still hidden:
     - **`AssetStore::new` (`:128`) runs `fontdb::Database::new();
  load_system_fonts()` on the UI thread**
  (`arvane-asset/src/store/asset_store.rs:83-84`). It also spawns the
  `arvane-asset` thread (`:72`, `worker.rs:95`).
       - fontdb 0.23 walks `%SYSTEMROOT%\Fonts` and two per-user font folders,
  memory-mapping and parsing every face
  (`REG\fontdb-0.23.0\src\lib.rs:400-421`). On this machine that folder holds
  **614 files, 697 MB**.
       - It is only used for SVG `<text>` (`asset_store.rs:45-47`), and it is a
  second full system-font scan after fontique's.
       - It runs eagerly, whether or not the app uses any SVG. This is the most
  likely largest synchronous startup cost.
     - `Host::begin` (`events.rs:45-78`) → client `Launch::start`
  (`kivact-core/src/launch/runtime.rs:157-216`):
       - The DevTools agent starts only with feature `devtools`: TCP bind,
  discovery file and a thread (`kivact-devtools-agent/src/agent.rs:46-76`).
       - The blocking pool is created on first use
  (`kivact-runtime/src/task/state.rs:285-299`), default `min(4, CPUs)` threads
  (`worker_pool.rs:60-64`).
       - tokio starts `NotStarted` (`state.rs:131`).
       - The async executor is `async_task::spawn_local` on the UI thread, so it
  adds no threads (`executor.rs:3,62-64`).
     - `start_mount` (`runtime.rs:115-156`):
       - App fonts are registered into both parley and fontdb
  (`kivact-engine/src/lower/assets.rs:253,262`); fontdb copies the bytes
  (`asset_store.rs:159`).
       - `font_families()` clones and sorts every system family name
  (`runtime.rs:131`, `text_engine.rs:106-120`).
       - The locale is just a tag memo; no ICU runs
  (`view/root.rs:53-61,110-112`).
       - Then the root mounts and commits.
     - `settle` (first layout and shaping), the turns, then `request_redraw`
  (`window_changes.rs:198-201`).
     - No library crate installs a tracing subscriber (no `tracing_subscriber`
  or `set_global_default` anywhere).
  7. **`set_visible(true)` runs right after the start callback**
  (`runtime.rs:486-488`). The first frame comes later, on `RedrawRequested`
  (`window_events.rs:202`). It is drawn on the CPU
  (`arvane/src/window.rs:265,421`) into a softbuffer surface created at the
  first present (`arvane-native/src/present.rs:32-44`).
     - **So the window is shown before its first frame.** Layout is already
  done, but nothing is painted yet.
     - winit's window class has `hbrBackground: 0`
  (`REG\winit-0.30.13\src\platform_impl\windows\window.rs:1419`), and the winit
  port has no `WM_ERASEBKGND` or DWM cloak handling. A blank/white flash is
  likely.
  8. On the GPU wake, the UI thread makes the wgpu surface
  (`arvane/src/window.rs:447-455`) and the **`arvane-gpu-configure`** worker
  sets up the swapchain (`gpu.rs:613-631`). Frames stay on the CPU until it is
  ready.

  **ICU4X** is fully lazy and never cached. Each call builds a new formatter or
  `Collator` from compiled data
  (`kivact-i18n/src/regional.rs:31-33,47-50,176,189-192`,
  `numbers.rs:42-45,61-63`, `locale.rs:94-97`). That costs nothing at startup,
  but `Locale::compare` builds a Collator on every comparison.

  ## Part B — Binary size

  ### (1) Heavy crates in the default Windows tree
  From `cargo tree --locked --offline -p kivact --target
  x86_64-pc-windows-msvc`:

  - **GPU:** wgpu, wgpu-core and wgpu-hal 29.0.4 with vulkan, dx12 and metal
  (metal is compiled out on Windows) (root `Cargo.toml:82`). This brings ash,
  gpu-allocator, `windows` 0.62 and renderdoc-sys. There is no GLES backend (no
  glow).
  - **naga 29.0.4:** wgsl-in, spv-out, hlsl-out, plus the spirv crate.
  - **Renderers:** vello_cpu, vello_common and vello_hybrid 0.2 (plus
  vello_sparse_shaders, which ships WGSL source), glifo, fearless_simd, peniko,
  kurbo 0.13.1.
  - **Text stack 1:** parley 0.11.1 with `complex-scripts` (`Cargo.toml:69`),
  fontique, harfrust, skrifa 0.44, read-fonts 0.41, parley_data, and
  icu_segmenter, icu_properties and icu_normalizer with compiled data.
  - **Text stack 2, from resvg/usvg 0.46** (`arvane-asset/Cargo.toml:17`,
  features text, system-fonts, raster-images): fontdb 0.23, rustybuzz 0.20.1,
  ttf-parser 0.25.1, unicode-bidi/script/ccc/vo, roxmltree 0.21, simplecss, and
  **tiny-skia 0.11.4 as a third rasterizer**, plus png 0.17 and memmap2.
  - **Images:** image 0.25.10 with png 0.18, zune-jpeg, gif, image-webp and
  moxcms (`arvane-asset/Cargo.toml:13`); flate2 1.1.10.

  (`unstable`, ixdtf), decimal, plurals, locale_core, locale_fallback, time and

  dead-stripped since only YMD/T fieldsets are used, unverified); segmenter_data
  9.3 MB (dictionary_auto 5.2 MB, extended 2.9 MB, lstm 0.9 MB); collator_data
  3.2 MB (tailoring 2.8 MB); properties_data 2.0 MB.
    - `complex-scripts` makes parley call `new_dictionary`
  (`REG\parley-0.11.1\src\analysis\mod.rs:47-49,99-103`), which pulls in the
  segmenter dictionaries.
  - **Platform:** winit 0.30.13, softbuffer 0.4.8, accesskit 0.24.1 with
  accesskit_consumer 0.37 and accesskit_windows 0.33.1, arboard 3.6.1, rfd
  0.17.2, sys-locale.

  - **serde/serde_json are not in the default Windows build.** They come only

  - **naga is linked even without `gpu`.** `cargo tree --no-default-features -i
  naga` shows kivact-shader pulled by both arvane-compose and kivact-style.

  ### (4) Release profile
  There is no `[profile.release]` anywhere. The root `Cargo.toml` only has
  `[profile.dev.package.*]` overrides (`:114-192`), and `.cargo/config.toml`
  only defines an xtask alias. So lto, codegen-units, panic, strip and opt-level
  are all at Cargo's defaults.

  - `arvane/src/test_font.rs:7`: Tuffy.ttf, 172,324 B, in the library crate. It

› Message from Explore
  ## Census of implicit state machines in kivact

  I found 78 places where a component's phase is spread across several signals,
  fields or machines, plus 6 recurring patterns behind most of them. None of the
  target crates had been changed; this was a read-only sweep.

  ### The canonical pattern and what the lint catches
  - **Machine** (`crates/kivact-runtime/src/machine.rs`): `transition(&self,
  &State, Event) -> Step`. `Step` can stay, move to a new state, attach effects
  with `.with`, and set or cancel timers with `.after`/`.cancel` (one timer per
  event variant). `input` builds a fresh snapshot for each event. `feed(source,
  f)` turns changes of a value into events.
  - **Lint** (`dev/xtask/src/state_fields/{check,classify,locals}.rs`):
  confirmed that it only checks structs, enum variants with named fields, and
  statics/thread_locals. It errors when there are 2 or more bool/Option/counter
  fields, or an Option next to a `*State`/`*Phase` enum or a `MachineHandle`.
    - **Locals are not enforced.** `locals.rs` runs only with `--report` and
  prints `state-locals:` notes that never fail the build. It sees
  `signal(bool|int literal|None)`, `Cell::new`, `Rc::new(Cell::new(..))` and
  `RefCell::new(None)`.
    - **Locals it misses:** `signal(0.0)`, `signal(Some(..))`,
  `signal(Point..)`, `var(..)`, `owner.signal(..)` and
  `Rc::new(RefCell::new(None))` (e.g. devtools `scripts.rs:85`).
    - **No cluster analysis:** each local is reported on its own.
    - **Not inspected at all:** function parameters (e.g. `overlays(confirm,
  form, sheet, command: Signal<bool>)`), tuple variants, and `type State =
  Option<..>`.
    - **Exempt by policy:** type names ending in
  Options/Event/Phase/Reason/Input/Report/Tokens/Recipes, and the
  `named_helpers` (`Generation`, `Sent`, …), so epoch counters and `Sent` caches
  are never flagged.
  - **effect-writes lint** (`effect_writes.rs`): only checks `Signal`/`Var`
  receivers under `crates/`. It skips examples and `Binding` writes, and allows
  any machine `.send` from an effect. That is why `combobox/handle.rs:386` and
  `game/system.rs:51,114` pass.

  Priority: H = high, M = medium, L = low.

  ### Recurring patterns (fixing these covers most rows)
  - **P1 – shadow state next to a drag:** a drag session plus a separate signal
  or machine tracking the same gesture. Found in U7, U10, C4, C5, C8, C9, C10,
  S4. Fix: a `use_drag` variant that forwards Start/Update/End/Cancel into one
  domain machine.
  - **P2 – caller's `open` binding plus an internal machine:** I1, I2, U1, U2,
  U4, U8, S8, S9. Fix: the machine owns the phase and the binding becomes a
  projection (`feed` in, effect writes out).
  - **P3 – timestamps that expire lazily:** I5, I6. Fix: `Step::after`.
  - **P4 – `Sent` caches next to machines:** I7, I8, U5. Fix: move the cache
  into the machine state.
  - **P5 – `Generation` stale-work guards:** I10, C6, C7. Fix: the `Registry`
  helper.
  - **P6 – clocks started/stopped by effects:** G1, E7, E8, while button.rs and
  progress.rs already do this with machines. Fix: one shared motion-clock
  machine.

● Update(SLIM_FAST_DESIGN.md)

      587  
      588  公開 API（`style().padding(24).gap(12)` などの builder の meth
           od 名と引数、`Style::merge`、`compile_style` 等の hidden bridg
           e の署名）は変えない。内部表現だけを変える。
      589  
      590 -- 今の `pub struct Style { ...約 60 field... }`（`sheet.rs:177
          -`）を `pub(crate) struct StyleFields`（同じ field、同じ型、`De
          -fault`）に改名する。`compile_style`・`resolve.rs`・`text.rs`  
          -など field を読むコードは `StyleFields` を読む。              
      591 -- 新しい `Style` は宣言の列。                                 
      590 +- 今の `pub struct Style { ...約 60 field... }`（`sheet.rs:177
          +`）を `pub(crate) struct StyleFields`（同じ field、同じ型、同 
          +じ derive）に改名する。今の `Style::merge` の本体は `StyleFiel
          +ds::merge(mut self, o: &StyleFields) -> StyleFields` に移す（ 
          +中身は変えない）。`compile_style`・`resolve.rs`・`text.rs` な 
          +ど field を読むコードは `&StyleFields` を受け取る。           
      591 +- 新しい `Style` は「builder の連鎖ごとの宣言の層」の列である 
          +。                                                            
      592  
      593  ```rust
      594 -/// An authored style: the declarations in the order they were
          - written                                                      
      595 -/// (a later one of the same property wins). Small: a plain bo
          -x with a                                                      
      596 -/// size is two declarations.                                 
      597 -#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq)]                   
      594 +/// One setter call, replayed on the dense fields.            
      595 +type Decl = std::rc::Rc<dyn Fn(&mut StyleFields)>;            
      596 +                                                              
      597 +/// An authored style: the setter calls of each builder chain 
          +(a layer), in                                                 
      598 +/// order; `merge` appends the other style's layers. Its value
          + is the                                                       
      599 +/// layers' dense fields merged in order, exactly as the dense
          + style merged.                                                
      600 +/// Small: a box with a size is one layer of two calls.       
      601 +#[derive(Clone, Default)]                                     
      602  pub struct Style {
      599 -    decls: Vec<Decl>,                                         
      603 +    layers: Vec<Vec<Decl>>,                                   
      604  }
      605  
      602 -/// One declared property (one variant per `StyleFields` field
          -).                                                            
      603 -#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]                            
      604 -pub(crate) enum Decl { /* generated */ }                      
      605 -```                                                           
      606 +impl Style {                                                  
      607 +    /// Record one setter call in the last layer (a new style 
          +starts one).                                                  
      608 +    fn edit(mut self, decl: impl Fn(&mut StyleFields) + 'stati
          +c) -> Self {                                                  
      609 +        if self.layers.is_empty() {                           
      610 +            self.layers.push(Vec::new());                     
      611 +        }                                                     
      612 +        if let Some(layer) = self.layers.last_mut() {         
      613 +            layer.push(std::rc::Rc::new(decl));               
      614 +        }                                                     
      615 +        self                                                  
      616 +    }                                                         
      617  
      607 -- `Decl` と変換は一つの macro で `StyleFields` の field の一覧
          -から生成する（field ごとに variant 一つ、payload は field の中
          -身の型。`Option<T>` の field は `T`、`Sides` のような field は
          - `Sides` のまま）。                                           
      618 +    /// The other style's layers after this one's (the dense m
          +erge, per layer).                                             
      619 +    pub fn merge(mut self, o: &Style) -> Style {              
      620 +        self.layers.extend(o.layers.iter().cloned());         
      621 +        self                                                  
      622 +    }                                                         
      623  
      609 -```rust                                                       
      610 -macro_rules! style_fields {                                   
      611 -    ($( $field:ident : $ty:ty => $variant:ident ),* $(,)?) => 
          -{                                                             
      612 -        pub(crate) enum Decl { $( $variant($ty), )* }         
      613 -        impl Decl {                                           
      614 -            /// Write this declaration into the dense fields. 
      615 -            pub(crate) fn apply(self, f: &mut StyleFields) { m
          -atch self { $( Decl::$variant(v) => f.$field = v.into(), )* } 
          -}                                                             
      616 -        }                                                     
      617 -    };                                                        
      624 +    /// The dense value: each layer replayed on the default, m
          +erged in order.                                               
      625 +    pub(crate) fn fields(&self) -> StyleFields {              
      626 +        self.layers.iter().fold(StyleFields::default(), |merge
          +d, layer| {                                                   
      627 +            let mut dense = StyleFields::default();           
      628 +            for decl in layer {                               
      629 +                decl(&mut dense);                             
      630 +            }                                                 
      631 +            merged.merge(&dense)                              
      632 +        })                                                    
      633 +    }

      619 -```                                                           
      635  
      621 -（`Option<T>` の field には `Some(v)` を書く。macro の各行で `
          -=> Some` か `=> plain` を区別する。具体的な書き方は作業指示書 
          -U15 に従う。）                                                
      636 +impl PartialEq for Style {                                    
      637 +    fn eq(&self, other: &Self) -> bool {                      
      638 +        self.fields() == other.fields()                       
      639 +    }                                                         
      640 +}                                                             
      641  
      623 -- 各 setter（今の `self.width = Some(v)` など）は `self.push(D
          -ecl::Width(v))` にする。`push` は同じ variant の古い宣言を残し
          -てよい（後勝ち）。`Style` が 1 property を何度も書き換える使い
          -方は少ないので、置換はしない。                                
      624 -- `Style::merge(&self, other: &Style) -> Style` は `decls` の 
          -連結（self の後に other）。                                   
      625 -- `Style::fields(&self) -> StyleFields`（crate 内）は `StyleFi
          -elds::default()` に順に `apply` する。`compile_style` などは最
          -初にこれを呼ぶ。                                              
      626 -- `size_of::<Style>()` は 24 byte になる。                    
      642 +impl std::fmt::Debug for Style {                              
      643 +    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt
          +::Result {                                                    
      644 +        self.fields().fmt(f)                                  
      645 +    }                                                         
      646 +}                                                             
      647 +```                                                           
      648  
      649 +- 各 setter は、引数の変換（`self` に触れない部分）を先に行い 
          +、`self` への代入を `edit` の closure に移す。closure の中では
          + `self.` を `s.` に読み替え、捕まえた値は毎回 `.clone()` する 
          +（closure は何度も呼ばれる）。例：`pub fn width(self, w: impl 
          +Into<Length>) -> Self { let w = w.into(); self.edit(move |s| s
          +.width = Some(w.clone())) }`。一つの setter が複数の field を 
          +書く場合も、同じ代入を closure の中にそのまま写す。           
      650 +- 正しさの理由：一つの builder の連鎖の結果は、今の dense な `
          +Style` に同じ setter を順に呼んだ結果と同じ（層の replay）。`m
          +erge` の結果は、今の `merge` を層ごとに順に適用した結果と同じ 
          +（`fields()` の fold）。したがって `compile_style` に渡る dens
          +e な値は今と一致する。                                        
      651 +- `Style` は今も `local_paint: Option<LocalPaint>`（`Rc`）を持
          +つので `Send` ではない。`Rc` の decl は `Send` の性質を変えな 
          +い。                                                          
      652 +- `size_of::<Style>()` は 24 byte になる。box の大きさを設定す
          +る style は、heap に層 1 つ（2 decl）で約 160 byte。          
      653 +                                                              
      654  #### MEM-2 `StateStyles` を必要な時だけ持つ（kivact-types、cor
           e）
      655  
      656  - `kivact-types/src/paint.rs` の `StateStyles` の各 field `Opt
           ion<PaintDelta>` を `Option<Box<PaintDelta>>` にする（`hover`
           、`pressed`、`focus`、`focus_visible`、`group_hover`、`group_f
           ocus`、`enter`、`exit`）。`deltas()` は `.as_deref()` を通す。

      189  - **K1**：B0 の 390 ms のうち 282 ms が R-1、約 69 ms が R-2 
           である。両方を除くと window 作成 23 ms、mount と layout 約 10 
           ms、最初の font 読込みと shaping、raster と present が残る。R-
           2 の高速経路で 800×600 の全画面 frame は 5〜10 ms に下がる（ve
           llo_cpu の塗りと行の複写）。合計 50〜65 ms。
      190 -- **K2**：既定 renderer のアイドル WS の大半は GPU ドライバで 
          -ある。GPU を必要時に起動する（R-3）と、counter のような画面は 
          -CPU の WS（47 MiB）になり、さらに R-1（system font の DB）と R
          --8 で約 30 MiB に下がる。item あたりは 1.2 節の割当て元から積 
          -み上げた。空 box は `StateStyles` 5.9 KB、`resolve` の残留、`S
          -tyle` 1.9 KB、`ComputedTextStyle` 0.47 KB がほぼ消え、layout  
          -列（約 1〜2.7 KB）と `LayoutProps` 0.7 KB と `HostNode` が残る
          -ので 4〜5 KB（6〜7×）。button は reactive node を 40 から 12  
          -以下にし（R-10）、3 ノード分の状態 style と resolve の残留を除
          -く。                                                          
      190 +- **K2**：既定 renderer のアイドル WS の大半は GPU ドライバで 
          +ある。GPU を必要時に起動する（R-3）と、counter のような画面は 
          +CPU の WS（47 MiB）になり、さらに R-1（system font の DB）と R
          +-8 で約 30 MiB に下がる。item あたりは 1.2 節の割当て元から積 
          +み上げた。空 box は `StateStyles` 5.9 KB、`resolve` の残留、`S

          +列（約 1〜2.7 KB）と `LayoutProps` 0.7 KB と `HostNode` が残る
          +ので 4〜5 KB（6〜7×）。button は reactive node を 40 から 12  
          +以下にし（R-10）、状態 style を box の delta だけにし（hover・
          +pressed・focus_visible の `PaintDelta` 約 2.2 KB は残る）、res
          +olve の残留と recipe の `Style` 3 層（5.7 KB）を除いて 18〜22 
          +KB（5〜6×）。                                                 
      191  - **K3/K4**：3000 行で scene が 7.1 ms、毎 frame の O(N) は R-
           4（layout の丸め・比較・走査）と R-5（`layer_facts`）である。
           これを O(changed) にすると、見えている 30 行程度の lower と ra
           ster（約 1 ms）が残る。
      192  - **K5**：試験 driver は a11y を毎回公開する。R-7 で geometry 
           だけの変化は identity metadata を作り直さない経路にするが、走
           査自体（全ノードの stack 移動）は O(N) のまま残るため、無関係
           な更新でも 3000 行で 2〜4 ms は残る。wheel は scroll container
            の subtree の a11y payload を作り直すので、改善は小さい（1.5 
           節）。
      193  - **K6**：profile だけで 14.5 MB（1.7×）。速度に効く crate だ
           け opt-level=3 に戻す（4.8 節）ので既定 feature で約 14 MB。`c
           omplex-scripts` を外せる構成で約 11 MB。

      1356 +### B.2 一括置換の script（U31 手順 2）                      
      1357 +                                                             
      1358 +repository の root で、Git Bash か Linux の bash で実行する。
           +`grep -rlE` で対象を選び、対象が無い置換は何もしない。       
      1359 +                                                             
      1360 +```bash                                                      
      1361 +set -euo pipefail                                            
      1362 +                                                             
      1363 +# 置換の対象：U32〜U35 の crate と kivact-style を除いたすべ 
           +ての Rust と Markdown と TOML                                
      1364 +targets() {                                                  
      1365 +  git ls-files -- \                                          
      1366 +    'crates/kivact/*' 'crates/kivact-core/*' 'crates/kivact-t
           +est/*' 'crates/kivact-interaction/*' \                       
      1367 +    'crates/kivact-ui/*' 'crates/kivact-ui-basic/*' 'crates/k
           +ivact-ui-pop/*' 'crates/kivact-ui-game/*' \                  
      1368 +    'crates/kivact-ui-creative/*' 'crates/kivact-ui-soft/*' '
           +crates/kivact-devtools/*' \                                  
      1369 +    'crates/kivact-macros/*' 'crates/kivact-engine/*' \      
      1370 +    'examples/*' 'templates/*' 'dev/tests/*' 'dev/bench/*' 'd
           +ev/xtask/*' 'docs/*' \                                       
      1371 +    'README.md' 'README_JP.md' 'CONTRIBUTING.md' \           
      1372 +  | grep -E '\.(rs|md|toml)$'                                
      1373 +}                                                            
      1374 +                                                             
      1375 +# 1. 試験の locator（kivact-test の中は Element がすべて loca
           +tor）                                                        
      1376 +grep -rlE '\bElement\b' crates/kivact-test/src | xargs -r sed
           + -i -E 's/\bElement\b/Locator/g'                             
      1377 +# kivact-test の外：path 付き・lifetime 付き・1 行の use の中
           +・doc の `Element::`                                         
      1378 +targets | grep -v '^crates/kivact-test/' | xargs -r sed -i -E
           + \                                                           
      1379 +  -e 's/\b(kivact::test|kivact_test)::Element\b/\1::Locator/g
           +' \                                                          
      1380 +  -e "s/\bElement<'/Locator<'/g" \                           
      1381 +  -e 's/(use kivact(_test)?::test::\{[^}]*)\bElement\b/\1Loca
           +tor/g' \                                                     
      1382 +  -e 's/`Element::/`Locator::/g'                             
      1383 +                                                             
      1384 +# 2. NodeBuilder -> Element                                  
      1385 +targets | xargs -r sed -i -E 's/\bNodeBuilder\b/Element/g'   
      1386 +                                                             
      1387 +# 3. Component trait -> Element（Rust の型の位置だけ）       
      1388 +targets | grep -E '\.rs$' | xargs -r sed -i -E \             
      1389 +  -e 's/\bimpl Component\b/impl Element/g' \                 
      1390 +  -e 's/(: *|\+ *)Component\b/\1Element/g' \                 
      1391 +  -e 's/\bview::Component\b/view::Element/g'                 
      1392 +targets | grep -E '\.md$' | xargs -r sed -i -E \             
      1393 +  -e 's/\bimpl Component\b/impl Element/g' \                 
      1394 +  -e 's/`Component`/`Element`/g'                             
      1395 +                                                             
      1396 +# 4. 関数 component(..) -> scope(..)（closure を渡す呼び出し 
           +と path だけ。`.component(` の method は変えない）           
      1397 +targets | xargs -r sed -i -E \                               
      1398 +  -e 's/\bview::component\b/view::scope/g' \                 
      1399 +  -e 's/(^|[^.:A-Za-z0-9_])component\((move )?\|\|/\1scope(\2
           +||/g'                                                        
      1400 +sed -i -E 's/pub fn component<V: IntoView>/pub fn scope<V: In
           +toView>/' crates/kivact-core/src/view/boundary.rs            
      1401 +                                                             
      1402 +# 5. 確認（出力は U31 の報告に貼る）                         
      1403 +targets | xargs grep -nE '\bNodeBuilder\b|\bimpl Component\b|
           +view::component\b' || true                                   
      1404 +```                                                          
      1405 +                                                             
      1406 +置換の後、`use` の並びに `Element` が二重に入った箇所（旧 `No
           +deBuilder` と旧 `Component` の両方を import していた箇所）は 
           +、compile error（`the name Element is defined multiple times`
           +）が出るので、一つを消す。

● Update(SLIM_FAST_WORK_ORDER.md)
  ⎿  Added 1 line, removed 1 line
      868  手順：
      869  
      870  1. `kivact-types/src/paint.rs` の `StateStyles` の 8 つの `Opt
           ion<PaintDelta>` を `Option<Box<PaintDelta>>` にする。`deltas(
           )` の中の `[&self.hover, ..].into_iter().flatten()` を `[self.
           hover.as_deref(), ..].into_iter().flatten()` にする（戻り値の
           型 `impl Iterator<Item = &PaintDelta>` は変えない）。`StateSty
           les` の定義の直後に `const _: () = assert!(std::mem::size_of::
           <StateStyles>() <= 80, "StateStyles keeps its deltas boxed");`
            を足す。
      871 -2. `StateStyles` を作る・読む箇所（`rg -n "hover:|pressed:|foc
          -us_visible:|group_hover:|group_focus:|\.hover\b|\.pressed\b|\.
          -enter\b|\.exit\b" crates/kivact-style/src/resolve.rs crates/ki
          -vact-engine/src`）を `Box` に合わせる（作る側は `Some(Box::new
          -(delta))`、読む側は `.as_deref()`）。`kivact-engine` の中の追 
          -従は所有外でも行ってよい（W3 の他の unit は engine を所有しな 
          -い）。                                                        
      871 +2. `StateStyles` を作る・読む箇所（`grep -rnE "hover:|pressed:
          +|focus_visible:|group_hover:|group_focus:|\.hover\b|\.pressed\
          +b|\.enter\b|\.exit\b" crates/kivact-style/src/resolve.rs crate
          +s/kivact-engine/src`）を `Box` に合わせる（作る側は `Some(Box:
          +:new(delta))`、読む側は `.as_deref()`）。`kivact-engine` の中 
          +の追従は所有外でも行ってよい（W3 の他の unit は engine を所有 
          +しない）。                                                    
      872  3. MEM-5：`measure/inherited_text.rs` の `computed_text`：`let
            resolved = Rc::new(text_props(tree, id));` の前に、ノードが自
           分の `TextStyle` を持たない（`n.text()` 相当が `None`）なら、`
           let value = text_props(tree, id);`、親（`tree.parent_id(n)`）
           の `computed_text(tree, columns, parent)` を得て、`*parent_rc 
           == value` なら `Rc::clone(&parent_rc)` を `columns.set_compute
           d_text(..)` に入れて返す。等しくなければ今どおり `Rc::new(valu
           e)`。
