✻ Churned for 1m 58s · done 20:01 (月曜日)

› Message from general-purpose
  I checked about 30 claims against the code. Most file and line references are
  correct. The defects below are the ones that would stop a weak implementer or
  give wrong results. Nothing was modified.

  **BLOCKER**

  1. **WO 1.1, worktree setup.** `dev/xtask` is a workspace member with a path
  dependency on `vendor/xtask-core/...`, and that directory is a git submodule
  (`.gitmodules`). A fresh `git worktree add` does not check submodules out, so
  cargo cannot load the workspace and every `cargo check -p X` fails in every
  worktree. **Fix:** after the worktree add, insert `git -C ../kv-wt/<unit>
  submodule update --init`.

  2. **U11 step 8 (SvgFonts `OnceLock`) and U14 GPU-2 (`OnceCell` pipelines).**
  `clippy.toml` lists `std::sync::OnceLock`, `std::cell::OnceCell`, `LazyCell`
  and `LazyLock` under `disallowed-types`, and the workspace sets
  `disallowed_types = "deny"`. Rule 0.2.5 forbids adding `#[expect]`. So both
  units fail their own `cargo clippy` completion. This also contradicts ADR-17
  ("internal OnceCell is limited to atomic construction") and S-7/SM-5, and the
  design's 8.1 does not list them as replaced. **Fix:** either explicitly
  authorise `#[expect(clippy::disallowed_types, reason = "built on first use,
  off the startup path (PERF-2)")]` on each field and on the `static EMPTY` (the
  same pattern as `kivact-runtime/src/reactive/data.rs:73-77`), and add
  ADR-17/S-7 to 8.1; or use `Mutex<Option<Arc<Database>>>`.

  3. **Unused `xtask-allow(state-fields)` markers are errors.**
  `state_fields/check.rs:297-313` reports a marker as "unused marker" when the
  current rules do not count its field.
     - These markers are on fields that are not counted today:
       - U13 `scaled: BTreeSet` (not counted even in strict mode).
       - U21 `stale_facts: StaleFacts` (the enum name does not end in
  State/Phase).
       - U11 `OnceLock`, U14 `OnceCell`.
       - U16's SM-11 rows on `ext` and `task` (`OnceCell`) and `awaiting`.
       - Every marker that W3–W5 add for fields only strict mode counts.
     - Effect: U13 and U21 fail their own `--only state-fields` completion, and
  CP1/CP2 `cargo xtask check --no-write` fail.
     - **Fix:** add to U12 step 3: "In `Current` mode, a marker is used if
  `Strict` would count its field." Remove the `scaled` marker from design 4.4
  and U13 step 1.

  4. **U21 (PNT-1) breaks `paint_self_check`.** In
  `paint/list/self_check.rs:7-82`, the reference rebuild calls `paint_list()` on
  `PaintList { ..PaintList::default() }`, which has empty `facts`. With
  `StaleFacts::Nodes({})` the facts stay empty, so the `debug_assert_eq!` fires
  in every debug E2E (the host self-check runs after each settle). The
  self-check also consumes the pending stale marks, which are then lost. The
  撤退規則 cannot fix this. **Fix:** in U21, `paint_self_check` saves
  `self.paint.stale_facts`, sets it to `StaleFacts::All` before each
  `paint_list()` call, and restores it at the end.

  5. **U23, MEM-2/MEM-3 writer side.** `bind_style` writes through
  `tree.update(id,
  PropValue::Layout(..)/Paint(..)/States(..unwrap_or_default()))`. The engine
  stores these unconditionally:
     - `set_layout`/`set_paint` use `boxed(Some(v), ..)`
  (`host/write/typed.rs:166-171`).
     - `set_states` stores `Some(states)` (`model/states.rs:147`).

     So the core has no way to write `None`. Those files belong to U26
  (`host/**`) and U21 (`model/**`) in the same wave. **Fix:** move the change to
  the engine. In `typed.rs` use `boxed((v !=
  LayoutProps::default()).then_some(v), ..)`, the same for Paint. In
  `states.rs:147` use `(states != StateStyles::default()).then_some(states)`.
  Assign it to U34 (W3) and drop U23 step 1.

  6. **Same-wave file conflicts.**
     - **W2:** the A-EC `Routed` row (U26) must also change
  `kivact-engine/src/model/keys.rs` and `model/pointer/{machine,shell}.rs`
  (owned by U21), plus `kivact-core/src/view/input.rs` and
  `view/mount/part_pointer.rs` (owned by U23).
     - **W3:** the A-AR `RawKey` row (U32) must also change
  `kivact-test/src/raw.rs` and `kivact-core/src/devtools/input.rs` (owned by
  U31), plus `kivact-engine/src/contract.rs` and `host/testing.rs`. The row's
  追従 list omits the core, contract and testing files.
     - **Fix:** move the `Routed` row to U43+U44 (W4) and the `RawKey` row to
  W4.

  **MAJOR**

  7. **WO 1.4, allowed-to-fail lists.**
     - **W3:** the list omits `kivact`. The facade depends on `kivact-ui` and
  `kivact-interaction`, which are allowed to fail, so after W3 `kivact` fails
  and is routed to U31, which cannot fix it.
     - **W4:** the list omits `kivact-devtools`, which U46 itself says cannot
  compile yet. U46 also cannot check its own code: cargo never compiles
  `kivact-devtools` when `kivact-ui-basic` fails.
     - **W5:** U56 is told to follow `kivact-devtools` along with U51's
  concurrent `kivact-ui-basic` API changes, which it cannot see.
     - **Fix:** add `kivact` to W3 and `kivact-devtools` to W4. Move U46 (A-DT)
  and the devtools follow-up to W6 (a new U63).

  8. **WO 1.3, fix-up flow contradicts itself.** Errors go back to the owning
  subagent "in the same worktree", but the next bullet deletes that worktree
  after merge. A worktree branched before the merge also lacks the other units'
  changes. **Fix:** "Fixes after merge are made in a new worktree branched from
  the current integration HEAD (`wt/<unit>-fix`)."

  9. **U14 `paint_fast` (CPU-1).**
     - **Double blending.** Damage rects are not disjoint:
  `plan/damage.rs:221-246` appends vanished-layer bounds and backdrop bounds
  without merging them. Looping over each rect src-over-blends translucent
  pixels twice. The generic loop tests `rects.iter().any(..)` once per pixel.
  **Fix:** for rect *k*, skip pixels inside `rects[..k]`, or test membership per
  pixel.
     - **Wrong variant name.** `Composite::SrcOver` does not exist; it is
  `Composite::SourceOver` (`cpu/blend.rs:21`). As written, the implementer will
  STOP.
     - **Degenerate polygon.** `pixel_rect` accepts a 4-point polygon with a
  repeated corner (a triangle) as a full rectangle. **Fix:** require the four
  distinct `(x, y)` combinations.
     - **Overflow.** Use `.min(255)` before `as u8`.

  10. **U21 step 9 (PNT-1) misses changes that alter facts.** Each of these
  leaves wrong layers in release builds and makes the debug assertion panic in
  tests.
      - **Hover and other state styles.** `drawn_look` uses `Look::now` →
  `effective_paint` with the active state deltas. Hovering a style-state node is
  handled by the `restyled` and `moved` locals (`sync.rs:163-166`), which are
  not `restack = true` assignments, so step 9.8 never reaches them. A hover
  filter, will-change or 3D transform then never becomes a layer.
      - **Motion ticks.** `ticks.rs:159` (`restack |= motion.is_active()`)
  changes opacity and transform every tick, not only at start and end.
      - **Hidden subtrees.** Hiding leaves the descendants' facts in the map.
  While a node is hidden its descendants' facts are removed, and on unhide
  `node_facts` reads defaults.
      - **`display: contents` changes.** `reflattened` (`apply.rs:259`) is not
  marked.
      - **Compile issue.** `Look` derives only `Clone, Copy, Debug`, so `kept !=
  Some(look)` does not compile.
      - **Fix:**
        - Stale each restyled id and its flat children in `sync`.
        - Use `stale_all_facts` at `ticks.rs:159`, in `reflattened`, and for
  hidden/visibility writes.
        - Add `PartialEq` to `Look`.
        - Require `refresh_facts` to live in `paint/list/walk.rs`: `layer.rs`
  may only use `lower` (`[module_rules.kivact-engine.uses]`).

  11. **U21 PNT-2.**
      - **Two more full clears.** `NodeTree::insert` (`node_tree.rs:415`) and
  `detach` (`:446`) also call `self.flat.get_mut().clear()`. The work order only
  replaces the one in `install`, so R-6 is unchanged.
      - **Borrow error.** The design's `forget_flat_around` holds
  `self.flat.get_mut()` while calling `self.get()` (E0502).
      - **Fix:** replace the clears at 415 and 446 with
  `forget_flat_around(parent)`. Use `let mut flat = self.flat.borrow_mut();`.

  12. **U22 Geometry path (A11Y-1).** The Metadata path also runs
  `expand_overlay_dirt` and `invalidate_boxless_ancestors` (`dirty.rs:88-90`).
  The Geometry path skips both, so overlays anchored inside moved or scrolled
  subtrees and the bounds of transparent ancestors go stale. **Fix:** compute
  `order = self.semantic_order()`, build `inherited` from
  `self.a11y.metadata.parents` as in lines 76-87, then call both expanders and
  `expand_relation_dirt` with the accepted metadata.

  13. **U24 (GPU-1).**
      - **GPU never starts from Deferred.** `Gpu::start` returns unless
  `State::Off` (`presenter/gpu.rs:347`). **Fix:** "accept `Off | Deferred{..}`".
      - **Secondary windows not covered.** `Windows::open` (`window.rs:224-226`)
  also calls `start_gpu`, and the work order only changes `Driver::start`.
      - **GPU tests fall back to CPU.** `GpuMode::Required` test hosts
  (`kivact-engine/src/host/testing/test_host.rs:203`,
  `arvane/tests/e2e/support.rs:390`, and `gpu_interop.rs`'s `.gpu()`) get
  `OnDemand` through `..Default::default()`. The GPU E2E then waits on CPU and
  fails, which also contradicts ADR-03. **Fix:** "stepped and virtual shells
  with `prefer_gpu` use AtLaunch".

  14. **U24 step 1, struct-literal search too narrow.** It greps only `crates`.
  `docs/for-kivact-user/{en,jp}/how-to-guide/gpu-texture-sharing.md:351/:284`
  build `GpuPreference {..}` without `..Default`, so CP1's `kivact-docs
  --all-features` fails. **Fix:** grep `crates dev docs examples templates` and
  allow those edits.

  15. **U16 A-RT `AnimInfo`.** `running`/`paused` are read at
  `kivact-core/src/devtools/motion.rs:37-38`, which is compiled only under
  `devtools`. U16's completion check has no `--all-features`, so this surfaces
  at CP1. **Fix:** allow that one file as 追従 and add `cargo check -p
  kivact-core --all-features`.

  16. **CP2 kivact-docs.** `dev/tests/docs` compiles every rust block in
  `docs/**` and the READMEs. The W4/W5 API changes (`alert.open`, positional
  `list`, `on_activate`, `show`, `ToolStatus`, `Focus`) are only reflected in
  docs in W6, so CP2 fails. **Fix:** in W5, each kit unit also owns
  `docs/for-kivact-ui-user/*/<kit>/**`, and U56 owns `docs/for-kivact-user/**`.

  17. **`kv_heap` breaks after U11.** The new `strip = "symbols"` removes the
  line tables that `kv_heap` resolves symbols from (`kv_heap.rs:118`). This
  breaks U23 step 4 and the K2 sites in `run.sh:22`. **Fix:** prefix both builds
  with `CARGO_PROFILE_RELEASE_STRIP=none`.

  18. **U15 MEM-1.**
      - **Nested state styles not specified.**
  `hover`/`pressed`/`focus`/`enter`/`exit` are `Option<Box<Style>>` inside the
  struct (`sheet.rs:324-332`), and the work order does not say what they become.
      - **Merge equivalence is false.** Setters called after `merge` go into the
  other style's last layer, so `a.merge(&b).hover(h)` replaces the hover in the
  dense code but merges it in the fold. The `duplicates` counts also differ.
      - **Fix:** store a single `Vec<Decl>`. `merge` pushes `move |s| *s =
  std::mem::take(s).merge(&o.fields())`, and `fields()` replays everything on
  one accumulator. This is exactly the dense semantics. Nested fields stay
  `Option<Box<Style>>`, and readers call `.fields()`.

  19. **U25 `#[component]` expansion.**
      - **Conversion at call time.** The positional function and the setters
  convert with `into_value`/`into_text_value` when called (rules 4-5). Per
  `props.rs:179-190`, constants and closures need the caller's setup owner:
  memos become owned by the caller, and calls outside setup panic with
  SetupOnly.
      - **No caller location.** There is no `#[track_caller]` (API-12).
      - **Children ignore the existing rule.** KIT-5 says children use
  `IntoChildren`; the macro maps them to `View`.
      - **Fix:** store the raw input as `Box<dyn FnOnce() -> Value<T>>` and
  convert inside `scope`. Emit `#[track_caller]`. Map `children` to
  `IntoChildren`.

  20. **Strict-lint coverage gap.** No W3–W5 unit owns `arvane-text` (41
  markers), `kivact-macros`, `dev/xtask`, `dev/tests/*` or `dev/bench`, yet CP2
  requires zero strict findings workspace-wide. **Fix:** add them to U45 and
  U56.

  21. **U62 completion grep cannot pass.** `decisions.md` necessarily keeps
  ADR-34's question ("外部 NodeBuilder 実装を閉じるか") and the SLIM-05 row
  ("NodeBuilder→Element"), so the "no output" check can never be met. **Fix:**
  exclude `docs/for-kivact-developer/decisions.md`.

  **MINOR**

  22. **CP3:** `dev/tools/test-wslg.sh` does not exist; `dev/tools/` has only
  `check-features.sh` and `window-lock.sh`.
  23. **U35:** step 1 says `hashbrown = "0.16"`, but parley uses 0.17.1
  (`Cargo.lock`). The step contradicts itself.
  24. **W2 real windows:** U25's e2e and U23's `kv_heap` both open windows,
  concurrently and without `dev/tools/window-lock.sh`. The script itself warns
  this can hang; wrap both.
  25. **U56 completion:** the workspace check and clippy need `--keep-going`.
  Otherwise kit errors stop the build and the other crates go unchecked.
  26. **MEM-6 premise:**
      - `part.rs:72` already builds one `PartState` memo.
      - `loading` (`button.rs:389`) is passed to `busy_machine.feed` and must
  stay reactive.
      - Design 4.7 says U46 measures the result, but U46 is the DevTools unit.
  27. **W0:** the work order says the branch already contains the docs,
  `dev/bench` and the R-0 fix, but they are uncommitted. `runtime.rs`,
  `workspace.toml` and `refresh.rs` also carry whole-file CRLF/LF churn.
  Normalise line endings and commit before U00.
  28. **Appendix A guesses:**
      - A-AR `Drawn` drops the `renderer` field (`frame_policy.rs:19`).
      - A-EN `Step.own/grouped/group` are not mutually exclusive.
      - GPU-2's "18 fields" is really `draw: Vec` (12) plus 6. The
  `PipelineLayout`s must become fields, which the work order does not say.
  29. **Naming and leftover text:**
      - LAY-1 calls the counter `pass: u32` in the design and `stamp` in the
  work order.
      - The facade description still says "no macros"
  (`crates/kivact/Cargo.toml`).
      - The comment above `kivact-shader` in `workspace.toml` (line 18) still
  names `arvane-compose`.

● 作業ブランチ refactor/kashiwade/slim-fast を作り、KPI
  の分析・設計書・作業指示書をリポジトリルートに置きました。ただしまだ commit も
  push もしていません。別マシンで使うには push
  が必要なので、最後に確認させてください。

  KPI の分析と目標（倍率は「改善前 ÷ 改善後」）

  このマシン（i9-14900K、Intel UHD
  770）で実測した現状と、根本原因から積み上げた目標です。別マシンでは最初の
  wave（W0）で同じ計測をやり直し（B0）、その値に対する倍率で判定します。

  KPI: 起動（main→初回描画）
  現状（実測）: 約 390〜440 ms
  必達: ≥5×
  見込み: 6〜8×
  主な原因: 起動時に全システムフォントを走査（282 ms）。CPU
  合成が全画素を汎用の浮動小数点ループで処理（約 63 ns/画素、初回フレーム 99
  ms）
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  KPI: メモリ（counter、アイドル）
  現状（実測）: 183〜196 MiB
  必達: ≥5×
  見込み: 6〜7×
  主な原因: GPU を常に起動時に初期化（+150〜250 MiB）
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  KPI: メモリ（3000 行の画面）
  現状（実測）: 611 MiB
  必達: ≥5×
  見込み: 6〜7×
  主な原因: 1 ノード分のスタイルを何重にも保持。button は 1 個約 117
  KB、reactive
  node が約 40 個、使わない busy 用の frame_loop も毎回作る
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  KPI: CPU（アイドル）
  現状（実測）: 0 frame、約 0 ms/s
  必達: 維持
  見込み: —
  主な原因: 既に良好
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  KPI: CPU（3000 行、毎フレーム更新）
  現状（実測）: 1 コアの 22〜25%
  必達: ≥5×
  見込み: 6〜8×
  主な原因: layout の丸めと比較、レイヤー判定が毎回全ノードを走査
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  KPI: フレーム時間 p95/p99（3000 行）
  現状（実測）: 9.5 / 10 ms
  必達: ≥6× / ≥5×
  見込み: 7〜9×
  主な原因: 同上
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  KPI: UI 更新レイテンシ（3000 行）
  現状（実測）: 無関係な更新 44〜72 ms、ホバー p95 63〜95 ms
  必達: ≥8×、≥10×
  見込み: 10〜25×
  主な原因: 同上と、a11y が全ノードを作り直すこと
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  KPI: バイナリ（counter）
  現状（実測）: 24.6 MB
  必達: ≥1.7×
  見込み: 約 14 MB
  主な原因: release の設定が無い。多言語の分かち書き辞書が約 3
  MB（外せる構成なら
  ≥2.2×）

  目標に入れていないものもあります。GPU を強制したときのメモリはドライバ次第です
  。支援技術が有効なときのスクロールは、a11y
  の座標の持ち方を変える必要があり範囲外にしました。

  調査の途中で分かった、現状のままでは困る点が 3 つあります。

  - main の release ビルドはコンパイルできませんでした。
    作業ブランチで修正済みで、debug・release・testing の 3
    構成とも警告ゼロです。
  - 通しの計測では、GPU 経路の kv_stress が 35 fps でした（単独で測ると 64
    fps）。GPU のフレーム時間は機械の他の負荷に左右されるので、計測は他の描画を
    止めて行う必要があります。
  - Git Bash の /proc/<pid>/winpid は別プロセスを指すことがあります。途中の CPU
    時間の計測がこれで一度ずれたため、計測 script
    はイメージ名で照合する形にしました。

  作ったもの

  - SLIM_FAST_DESIGN.md：上の KPI
    レポート、原因と証拠、設計の決定（SLIM-01〜07）。
    - crate の分割は、境界がはっきりした 2 つだけ追加しました。#[component] 用の
      proc-macro crate（Rust の規則で独立が必須）と、計測用の dev crate
      です。他の候補は理由を付けて分けていません。
    - 公開 API の変更：#[component] で props の builder を自動生成し（手書き約
      80 行が不要になる）、名前の衝突を解消しました（NodeBuilder→Element、compon
      ent()→scope()、試験の
      Element→Locator）。引数の規約（ARG-1〜6）も全部品で統一します。
    - ハンドラ型を 1 つの trait にまとめる案は採りませんでした。その方式だと
      on_edit(|phase| ..) の引数の型が推論されず、型注釈が必要になるためです。
  - SLIM_FAST_WORK_ORDER.md：W0〜W7 の作業手順です。
    - 各 wave は最大 6 ユニットで、同じ wave の中で同じファイルを触らないよう、
      ユニットごとに担当ファイルを決めてあります。
    - 重いテストはチェックポイント 3 回（CP1〜CP3）だけで、各ユニットは check と
      clippy、該当 lint だけを実行します。
    - 暗黙の状態機械は約 150 件を、直した後の enum の形まで付録 A
      に列挙しました。lint の抜け穴を塞ぐ規則も入れています。
    - 改名用の一括置換 script は、別の worktree で実際に動かし、kivact-core と
      kivact-test がコンパイルできるところまで確かめました。
  - dev/bench/：計測一式です（run.sh B0 で K1〜K6 を出力）。このマシンの参考値は
    dev/bench/results/analysis-machine.md にあります。

  独立したレビューで 29
  件の指摘があり、すべてコードで確認して反映しました。主なものは次のとおりです。

  - worktree で submodule が取得されず、cargo が失敗する
  - OnceCell/OnceLock が lint で禁止されている
  - 使われない lint の印が error になる
  - 同じ wave の中でファイルの取り合いがある（crate をまたぐ状態機械は W6
    の新ユニット U63 に移した）

  確認させてください

  1. commit と push をしてよいですか。 別マシンで作業ブランチを使うには push
     が必要です。
  2. リポジトリの外に、計測用の一時フォルダ（C:\w\kashiwade\kivact-bench、kivact
     -target-*）が残っています。数 GB あるので、不要なら削除します。

  7_CODEX_REVIEW.md" である。

  みにした結果、本来達成すべきだった下記に示す実施方針がブレてはいけない。      
                                                                                
  【実装方針】                                                                  
                                                                                
  - Rust 未経験のReact・SolidJS開発者がスムーズに書ける公開APIになっているか    
  - 公開APIはわかりやすく統一的なAPIであるか                                    
  - 公開APIは普段はJavaScriptやPythonを書いているRust未経験の人でも容易に記述で 
  きるか                                                                        
  - Kivact内部が統一的でシンプルな構造になっているか                            
  - Kivactの長期保守性                                                          
  - docs/for-kivact-developer/strategy.md を簡単に遂行できるか                  
                                                                                
  モジュール分割を行うよりはクレート分割を行うべきだ。ただし、クレート分割は目  
  的ではなく手段であり、境界がはっきりしていないのに分割してはいけない。        
                                                                                
  また、存在する暗黙的な状態マシンは全て解消せよ。

      951  ### 6.1 方針
      952  
      953 -1. xtask の `state-fields` の抜け穴を塞ぐ（6.2 節）。移行中は 
          -新しい検査を `state-fields-strict` という別名の rule にし、各 
          -wave の unit が自分の crate で 0 件にする。W7 で `state-fields
          -` に統合し、`state-fields-strict` を消す。                    
      954 -2. 見つかったものを、6.3 節の変換規則で機械的に直す。規則に当 
          -てはまらないものは「停止して報告」。                          
      955 -3. 分析で見つけた具体的な項目は作業指示書の付録 A に、直した後
          -の enum の形まで書いてある。                                  
      956 -                                                              
      953 +1. **監査が主、lint は補助。** 名前と型の構文を見る lint は、 
          +関数・object・crate をまたぐ意味の制約を網羅できない。そこで、
          +状態の持ち主・不変条件・有効な組合せ・event・初期化・期限・破 
          +棄を、作業指示書の付録 A に**直した後の型まで設計者が確定した 
          +表**として持つ。実装者はその表を写すだけにし、確かめてから設計
          +する手順（「確かめる、合わなければ STOP」）を残さない。       
      954 +2. lint の抜け穴を塞ぐ（6.2 節）。移行中は新しい検査を `state-
          +fields-strict` という別名の rule にし、各 wave の unit が自分 
          +の crate で 0 件にする。W6 で `state-fields` に統合し、`state-
          +fields-strict` を消す。                                       
      955 +3. 付録 A に無いもので lint が見つけたものは、6.3 節の規則のう
          +ち形がそのまま当てはまるものだけを実装者が直す。当てはまらない
          +もの、組合せの意味を読まないと決められないものは STOP し、設計
          +者（人間）が付録 A に行を足して確定する。STOP の項目は「未実施
          +」として完了扱いにしない（1.6 節 AC-3）。                     
      956 +4. 閉じ方は四つだけ：**型で表す**（enum、単一の Machine）、** 
          +導出して保持しない**、**単一の書き手の投影**（SM-12）、**真に 
          +独立**（SM-11、すべての組合せが有効と示せるもの）。           
      957 +5. 限界：静的な検査と台帳では、意味の上で隠れた状態を「全て」 
          +見つけたとは証明できない。完了の条件は「既知の項目が 0 件」と 
          +「監査の報告（6.4 節）を人間が確認した」ことである。

✻ Crunched for 22m 4s · done 22:04 (月曜日)

› Message from Explore
  ## 1. popup/machine.rs: `focused`, `focus_inside`
  1. **Declarations:** `crates/kivact-interaction/src/popup/machine.rs:81` has
  `focused: Option<NodeToken>` and `:83` has `focus_inside: bool`. Both are
  fields of the per-event input struct `Machine`.
  2. **Writes:** The only writer is `popup/api.rs:144-149`. The runtime calls
  this `input` closure fresh on every `send`
  (`kivact-runtime/src/machine.rs:180`, `(cell.input)()`), so neither field is
  retained. `focused` comes from `focused_node()`. `focus_inside` is
  `content.mounted_node().is_some_and(|p| content.focus_was_in(p))`.
  3. **Reads:** Only `may_restore` (`machine.rs:98`): `focused.is_none() ||
  focus_inside || target == focused`.
  4. **Reachable:** `focus_was_in_raw`
  (`kivact-core/src/view/node_ref.rs:452-456`) returns false when there is no
  focused node, and true when the focused node is gone from the tree or inside
  the panel. So `focus_inside` implies `focused.is_some()`, and `(None, true)`
  is unreachable. One caveat: `focused_node()` looks at the ambient owner's
  window while `focus_was_in` looks at the panel's window. They are only
  guaranteed equal when popup and panel share a window. When `focus_inside` is
  true, the token is never read.
  5. **Type:** `enum HostFocus { Nowhere, Inside, Elsewhere(NodeToken) }`, with
  `may_restore = !matches!(self, Elsewhere(t) if target != Some(t))`. `Inside`
  also covers "the focused node was removed". The type is `pub(super)`, so there
  is no public impact.

  ## 2. text_edit `next_control` / `is_mounted`
  1. **Declarations:** `handle.rs:505` `next_control: Cell<Generation>`
  (initialised at `:94`). `is_mounted` is at `:509-511`. The machine state is
  `machine.rs:10-16`: `Unmounted | Mounted { id, composition }`.
  2. **Writes:** `next_control` is written only at `handle.rs:434-436`. It
  advances once each time the `Part::new` build closure runs (one id per built
  control). It is never read anywhere else and never compared.
  3. **Reads / guard:** `is_mounted(id)` compares against
  `machine::State::Mounted{id}`. It is used at `:350` (text change), `:449`
  (Enter submit) and `:465` (after `Mount`, before autofocus and before
  registering the `Unmount` cleanup). The machine also guards `Unmount(id)` and
  `Composition{id}` by id (`machine.rs:38,47`). A second control gets
  `DuplicateMount` and its handlers stay dead. Closures from an earlier build
  are ignored.
  4. **Answer:** `next_control` is purely an id allocator. The stale-work guard
  is `machine::State::Mounted{id}`, which is already explicit.
  5. **Type:** Keep `Cell<Generation>` (or rename it to an id source). No new
  state type is needed. `Generation::is_current` is unused here.

  ## 3. list/state.rs: `typed`, `pending`, `roving.requested`, anchor
  They are four different facts, not one focus fact.
  - **`typed`** (`:56`, `Cell<Binding<Typeahead>>`, where `Typeahead = Idle |
  Typing{text, expires_ns}`):
    - The `Cell` is replaced at setup by `handle.rs:318`.
    - The value is written by `input.rs:221-223` (each typed char) and reset by
  `handle.rs:271`.
    - It is a time-windowed search buffer and holds no key.
  - **`pending`** (`:57`, machine state `{pending: Nothing | Activating{key,
  origin}, focus: Physical | Retained}`, `list/machine.rs:48-62`):
    - `Activate` is sent at `state.rs:240`, from `focus()` when the key is not
  mounted.
    - `Cancel` is sent at `:214`, `:279` and `:283`.
    - `Observe` at `:294` clears it when the origin was not kept.
    - `MountFocus` is sent at `parts.rs:156` on every item mount, from the
  `retained_focus` option.
  - **`roving.requested`** (`roving/registry.rs:68`, tab stop / highlight
  `Option<K>`):
    - Written at `state.rs:213`, `:284` and `:302`, and at `handle.rs:264`,
  `:270` and `:307` (binding replaced).
    - Also written at `input.rs:46` and `registry.rs:208` (adopts observed
  focus, only when `adopt`, i.e. pending is `Nothing`).
  - **anchor** (`ListSetupOptions.anchor: Binding<Option<K>>`, `:33`/`:85`, memo
  `:88`):
    - Written at `state.rs:172` (Single select) and `:193` (Multiple, via
  `selection_transition`), and by `handle.rs:184` `clear()`.
    - The binding is replaced by `with_anchor` (`handle.rs:135`); external
  writers can also write it.
  - **Combination reads:**
    - `active_value` `state.rs:337-343` combines requested, `pending.focus ==
  Physical`, scroll and membership.
    - `reconcile` `:298-310`: pending `Nothing` gates both the requested reset
  and adoption.
    - `focus_available` `:198-203` combines `retains_focus` and scroll.
    - `observe_list` `:411` reconciles only when `Physical`.
  - **Reachability:**
    - When it activates, `pending.key == requested` (`:213` runs before `:240`).
  But `requested` moves without a `Cancel` at `handle.rs:264/270`,
  `input.rs:46` and through an external shared binding. So `Activating{k}` with
  `requested ≠ Some(k)` is reachable, and `:284` re-asserts `k`. `pending.key`
  is therefore not derivable.
    - Anchor changes without `requested` changing via logical `key_selection`
  (`input.rs:254-271`), and `requested` changes without the anchor via
  `focus()`.
  - **Type:** Keep them separate. `pending.focus` is an orthogonal mount-time
  mode and could become its own field.

  ## 4. SwitchDragState (`kivact-ui/src/values.rs:245-260`, pub)
  - **Writer:** Only `State::visual()` in `switch_drag/machine.rs:93-126`.
  - **Readers:**
    - `kivact-ui-soft/src/recipes_controls_choice.rs:188-283`: `touch`,
  `tracking` (gates `visually_on`), `lifted`, `released`, `offset`.
    - `switch_drag/machine.rs:223` reads `.touch`.
  - **Reachable `(offset, touch, tracking, lifted, released)`** (5 of 32):
    - (a) touch `Pressed`: `(None, T, F, T, F)`
    - (b) touch horizontal drag: `(Some, T, T, T, F)`
    - (c) mouse/pen horizontal drag: `(None, F, T, F, F)`
    - (d) touch `Released{Raised}`: `(None, T, F, T, T)`
    - (e) touch `Released{Settled}`: `(None, T, F, F, T)`
    - Everything else maps to `None`: Idle, mouse Pressed, Vertical, Obscured,
  and non-touch Released.
  - **Invariants:**
    - `offset.is_some()` holds exactly when touch and tracking.
    - `!touch` implies (c).
    - `released` implies touch and not tracking.
  - **Type:**
    ```rust
    pub enum SwitchDragState {
        TouchPressed { visually_on: bool },
        Tracking { visually_on: bool, input: TrackInput /* Touch{offset: f64} |
  Pointer */ },
        TouchReleased { visually_on: bool, lift: Lift /* Raised | Settled */ },
    }
    ```
    `visually_on` in the non-Tracking variants is never read (recipes filter on
  `tracking`), so it could be dropped.
  - **Public API:** Breaking. The type is re-exported at
  `kivact-ui/src/lib.rs:190` and used in the pub `CheckableState.switch_drag`.

  ## 5. ChatComposerState (`chat_values.rs:98-103`, pub)
  - **Writer:** Only `chat_composer.rs:353-356`. It copies `stop_shown:
  Value<bool>` (caller, `:65`) and `status: Value<Option<ComposerStatus>>`
  (caller, `:79`). No internal writes.
  - **Reads:**
    - `status`: `look_chat.rs:108-112` and
  `kivact-ui-soft/src/recipes_chat.rs:179-181`.
    - `stopped`: no recipe reads it. The composer reads `stop_shown` directly at
  `chat_composer.rs:146, 203-222`.
  - **Combination reads:** none.
  - **Type:** Independent; all combinations are reachable because the caller
  drives both. Optionally rename `stopped` to `stop_shown` (it means "response
  in progress").

  ## 6. devtools `panel`, `popped`, `status`
  1. **Declarations:** `session.rs:29-31`; field types at `state.rs:71-76`.
  2. **Writes:**
     - `panel`: `app.rs:71` (tabs binding, user picks a tab), `session.rs:156`
  (pick hover), `:158` (pick select), `panels/review.rs:135` (go_to),
  `toolbar.rs:65` (Review button).
     - `popped`: `toolbar.rs:68-73` ("Pop out" pushes the current panel if
  absent) and `app.rs:122-124` (removed when its window closes).
     - `status`: `session.rs:203-205` (any RPC failure) and `:229` `report()`
  (from `:240`). It is never cleared.
  3. **Reads:** `app.rs:73` (panel), `app.rs:110-130` (popped),
  `status_bar.rs:26`. There are no combination reads.
  4. **Reachable:** Pop out does not change `panel`, so `panel ∈ popped` and
  `panel ∉ popped` are both reachable, and `status` is independent.
  5. **Type:** Independent. `popped` is a duplicate-free ordered set (invariant
  enforced at `toolbar.rs:70`), so a set type fits better than `Vec`.

  ## 7. dialog.rs / alert_dialog.rs `open`, `deleted`
  - **Declarations:** `dialog.rs:122-123` and `alert_dialog.rs:78-79`.
  - **`open` writes:**
    - Set true by the trigger (`dialog.rs:127`, `alert_dialog.rs:83`).
    - Set false by Cancel (`:141` / `:99`) and Delete (`:146` / `:104`).
    - The dialog binding can also close it.
  - **`deleted` writes:** Only `false → true` on Delete (`dialog.rs:145`,
  `alert_dialog.rs:103`). It is a monotonic latch.
  - **Reachability:** The trigger stays enabled after deletion, so `(open=true,
  deleted=true)` is reachable. All four combinations are reachable.
  - **Reads:** `deleted` only picks the text; `open` is only read by the dialog.
  - **Type:** Independent.

  ## 8. dock `Item::Chrome { shown, maximized }` (`dock/items.rs:10-17`,
  `pub(super)`)
  - **Writer:** Only `build_items` `:92-106`.
    - This container maximized gives `(FULL, shown=T, max=T)`.
    - Another container maximized gives `(frame, F, F)`.
    - Nothing maximized gives `(frame, T, F)`.
    - `(shown=F, max=T)` is unreachable.
  - **Reads:** `chrome.rs:61` (maximized) and `chrome.rs:120` via `Item::shown`.
  - **Type:** `Chrome { tabs, frame, display: ChromeDisplay }` with `enum
  ChromeDisplay { Normal, Maximized /* frame == FULL */, Hidden }`.
  `Item::shown()` becomes `display != Hidden`. No public impact.

  ## 9. node editor `DragMode::Link { rewire, released }` (`gesture.rs:190-198`)
  - **Writes:**
    - Pressing an output socket gives `rewire: None` (`input.rs:296-302`).
    - Pressing a linked input socket gives `rewire: Some(link)`
  (`input.rs:312-318`), with `from/output` copied from `link`, which is
  redundant.
    - `released` is set on any pointer Up (`input.rs:120-127`).
  - **Reads:**
    - `finish_fields` `input.rs:179-201` uses `released`, falling back to a
  proximity search, and then `write_links(rewire, add)`.
    - `rewired()` is used at `paint.rs:123`.
  - **Reachable:** All four `rewire × released` combinations. `released == None`
  means both "not released yet" and "released off-socket", but it is only read
  after release.
  - **Type:** `Link { source: LinkSource, to: Point, released: Option<(u64,
  usize)> }` with `enum LinkSource { Output { from, output }, Rewire(NodeLink)
  }`. This removes the `from/output` duplication; `rewire` and `released` stay
  independent.

  ## 10. creative shell `compact`, `sidebar_open`, `search`, `category`
  - **Declarations:** `shell.rs:816/818/823/827`, initialised at
  `:842/:843/:846/:850`.
  - **`compact` writes:** toggled at `:169` (density button) and `:724`
  (Ctrl+Alt+D).
  - **`sidebar_open` writes:** toggled at `:143`.
  - **`search` writes:** set true at `:175`, `:716` (F3) and `feedback.rs:383`;
  set false at `feedback.rs:234`, `:260` (on_dismiss) and `:270`.
  - **`category` writes:** only `Some(..)`, at `:158` (tabs) and `:481`
  (`Some(All)`).
  - **Reads:**
    - `compact`: `:168`, `:329`, `:546`, `:944-945`.
    - `sidebar_open`: `:935`.
    - `search`: `feedback.rs:202`.
    - `category`: `:157` `unwrap_or(All)` and `:441`, `:581` `is_some_and`,
  which treat `None` the same as `All`.
  - **Type:** The three bools are independent; every combination is reachable.
  `category`'s `None` is unreachable, so it becomes `Signal<Category>`.

  ## 11. side_navigation `ItemBranch::Parent { expansion, flyout }`
  (`items.rs:429-435`)
  - **Writer:** Only `ItemContext::new`.
    - `expansion` is `None` exactly when `ItemCollapse::AlwaysOpen` (`:503-516`,
  caller per item).

  shell-level).
  - **Reads:**

  flyout.
    - `activate` `:734-748`: flyout plus rail toggles the panel; otherwise the
  action runs; otherwise flyout plus expansion toggles `open` manually.
    - Also `:548` and `:675`.
  - **Reachable:** All four (AlwaysOpen/collapsible × collapsible-sidebar or
  not). Items inside a flyout get `collapsed = None`, hence no flyout.
  - **Type:** Independent; already exact.

  ## 12. SideNavCx { collapsed, popup } (`context.rs:8-14`, `pub(super)`)
  - **Constructions:**
    - `Default` gives `(None, None)`.
    - `shell.rs:240` gives `(collapsed?, None)`.
    - The flyout at `items.rs:809` gives `(None, Some(open))`.
    - Nested at `items.rs:763` copies both (`..cx`).
  - **Reachability:** `(Some, Some)` is never constructed, so it is unreachable.
  - **Reads:**
    - `collapsed`: `rail()`, `items.rs:518`, `:716`, `shell.rs:244`.
    - `popup`: `items.rs:738`.
  - **Type:** `enum NavSurface { Fixed, Collapsible(Binding<bool>), Flyout {
  open: Binding<bool> } }` plus `depth`. No public impact.

  ## 13. ToolStatus / ToolCall (`soft_chat_tools.rs:47-83`, all pub)
  - **Builders:**
    - `new` (`:88-99`) gives `Complete` with no duration or error.
    - `.status()` (`:100-103`) sets any status.
    - `.duration()` (`:111-114`) works with any status.
    - `.error()` (`:121-125`) sets `Error` plus `Some(msg)`.
    - The fields are pub, so any combination is constructible, e.g.
  `.error().status(Running)`.
  - **In-repo writers:** Only `Complete` with no extras
  (`examples/kivact-ui-soft/src/docs/collections.rs:356`, tests), `Running`
  (`:357`, `tests/consumer/chat_keyboard.rs:154`) and `Error` plus message
  (`chat_keyboard.rs:73`). Nothing in the repo calls `.duration()`.
  - **Rendering:**
    - Status label, spinner and glyph are at `:222-259`.
    - Duration is shown only when `status == Complete && duration.is_some()`
  (`:291`).
    - The error text shows whenever `error.is_some()`, regardless of status
  (`:722-732`).
  - **Type:** `enum ToolStatus { Pending, Running, Complete { duration:
  Option<Duration> }, Error { message: Option<String> } }`.
  - **Public API:** Breaking. It loses `Copy`, changes the builders, and drops
  the currently renderable "error text on a non-Error status" case.

  ## 14. DictationState (`chat_dictation.rs:18-29`, pub)
  - **Writers:** `Default` (all false/empty) and the only in-repo writer,

  volume: f64, bands: Vec<f64> } }`.
  - **Public API:** Breaking. It is re-exported at

  ## 16. TransportState (playing, recording)
  - **Declarations:** `audio/transport.rs:14-21` (pub) and the example copy
  `audio_values.rs:10-22`, bridged by `controls()` at `:35-54`.
  - **Writes:**

  - Type: `enum Run { Unassigned, Id(u64), Published { origin: Point, run:
  Arc<SemanticsTextRun> } }` (the id is `run.id.0`).

  **11. `MeasureColumns` / `editors`**
  - `natural`: set only at measure/leaf.rs:366, reached from the Image branch
  :221 when there is no editor; cleared by `take_slot` (state.rs:26); read at
  interact/sync.rs:250.
  - `extents`: set at leaf.rs:141-148, only from the Text branch :243 when there
  is no editor; cleared at leaf.rs:348 and in `take_slot`.
  - `computed_text`: set for any node at inherited_text.rs:43; forgotten at
  apply.rs:414, text.rs:70, `take_slot`.
  - `editors` (model/aggregate.rs:31): pushed None at lifecycle.rs:16; replaced
  through `set_edit` (inputs.rs:32), called from apply.rs:172, :544 and
  inputs.rs:45, :64; inner editor rewritten at inputs.rs:93; taken at
  lifecycle.rs:30, :69. It is keyed by the generic `Editing` prop, not by node
  kind; leaf.rs:218 lets an editor override any kind.
  - Kind never changes after creation (no `kind =` write found).
  - Answer: natural ⇒ Image and extents ⇒ Text, so those two are mutually
  exclusive. `editors` is not tied to kind. A stale natural/extents can sit next
  to an editor if Editing is added later (whether that happens in practice:
  uncertain). `computed_text` is independent.

  **12. `MouseTrack`** (pointer/state.rs:92 `over`, :93 `at`, :94 `hover`)
  - `at`: None at :223; set Some at shell.rs:70, :78, :110, :370 and
  state.rs:270. It never goes back to None. Every read is `unwrap_or_default`
  (state.rs:262, shell.rs:89).
  - `over`: set at machine.rs:253; set None at state.rs:437.
  - `hover.chain`: swapped at machine.rs:124 (from `set_chain`/`clear_hover`);
  trimmed with `retain` at state.rs:434.
  - `clear_hover` (e.g. shell.rs:162, leaving with a button held) empties the
  chain but keeps `over`.
  - Reachable: over Some with an empty chain; over None with a non-empty chain.
  - Type: `at: Point` loses nothing (nobody tells None from the default point).
  `over` and `hover` stay independent.

  **13. `SharedImage`** (gpu_interop/share/app_side.rs:27 `state`, :33
  `current`, :35 `pixels`)
  - `LinkState` writes: new :79; renew :174 (phase reset, token+1); close :110;
  take_work :189 (Provided → Importing); settle :227; engine side `provide`
  link.rs:225 and `put_pixels` :269.
  - `current`: None at :88 and :177 (renew); Some at :208 (import).
  - `pixels`: Some at :190 only; never cleared. The doc says "whatever the
  request" and `put_pixels` has no mode check.
  - Reachable while Open:
    - Waiting / Pixels / Provided ⇒ current None.
    - Importing / Ready / Failed ⇒ current Some with token == request token.
  Exception: the early return at :205 (no Runtime) would leave Importing with
  current None — uncertain whether reachable.
    - Ready may also show `ImportStatus::Lost` until the renewal effect runs.
  - `pixels` is independent of everything else.
  - Type: `current` is correlated with `Phase`, but the two live in different
  containers (Inbox shared with the engine thread vs a UI Signal), so
  `Option<Current>` is exact as split. `pixels` stays independent.

  **14. `deliver_pointer` delivery** (part_pointer.rs:431, :433)
  - The tuple comes from `select_pointer` :331-335. When origin ≠ node it
  returns `(None, false, false)` (:271).
  - deliver_raw = phase is not Enter/Leave. finish_fallback is true only for
  Cancel or an Up of the fallback button, so it implies deliver_raw, and also
  implies a target.
  - Reachable: (F,F) = foreign event or hover-only; (T,F) = raw; (T,T) = raw
  that ends a fallback gesture. (F,T) is impossible.
  - `delivered_finish`: starts false (:235); the only write is :472 `=
  finish_fallback`, made when the root RegionDefault fallback is reached; read
  at :247.
    - finish && !delivered: terminal event never reached the fallback → a Cancel
  is synthesized.
    - delivered && Err: an error came out of `fallback()?` (:473) after the flag
  was set → a Cancel is synthesized too.
    - delivered && Ok: nothing more.
  - Type: `enum Delivery { NoRaw, Raw, RawFinishingFallback }`, and return
  `FinishReached(bool)` instead of the `&mut bool`.

  **15. `XimConnection`** (arvane-native/src/port/x11/ime.rs:102)
  - `phase` writes: :574 `Handshake{0, None}`; :259-268 after each filtered
  ClientMessage, which yields `Established` whenever a server window is known.
  `Handshake` is therefore only ever built with `server_window: None`, and once
  Established it stays (the fallback at :257 returns Some).
  - `context` writes: :582 Connecting → :633 Open → :672 Ready{im,ic}; :792
  Disconnected, after which `filter` degrades in the same call (:249).
  - `flight` writes: :580 None; :352 `Live`; :379-393 `Canceled` (blur or
  disallow); :285 None on a matching SyncReply.
  - `pending` writes: :314 push, :326 pop, :334 clear, :361 push_front then
  degrade, :400 drain.
  - `degrade` drops all of it (:197).
  - Reachable at rest:
    - Disconnected is never observed between calls.
    - flight Some ⇒ context Ready.
    - pending non-empty ⇒ flight Some (`advance` loops until a flight is set or
  the queue is empty); (Canceled, non-empty) is reachable after refocusing.
    - Live ⇒ the App is allowed and focused.
  - Uncertain: whether Open/Ready implies Established depends on the xim crate's
  message order.
  - Type:
    - `Connection { Handshake { protocol_window: u32 }, Established {
  protocol_window: u32, server_window: u32 } }`
    - `Context { Connecting, Open, Ready { im, ic, keys: Keys }, Disconnected {
  keys: Keys } }`; Disconnected needs the keys because `degrade` hands them back
  to the keyboard.
    - `Keys { Idle, Waiting { flight: Flight, pending: VecDeque<(KeyPressEvent,
  bool)> } }`

  **16. `Entry.generation`** (arvane-asset/src/store/entry.rs:51)
  - Writes: `Entry::pending` (:195) via `start_load` (asset_store.rs:353-357,
  new value from `next_generation`, which starts at 1, :92); `reparse` :185-186
  (font registration, for Pending/Vector entries).
  - Stamped into jobs: Load :360 (new generation); `start_reload` :375 (the
  entry's current generation; used for the evicted-raster reload :259-260 and by
  reparse :188); Recheck :397; Raster :413-416.
  - Checked when results land: :432 (`apply_load`), :489 (Unchanged), :504
  (Raster).
  - A Recheck whose file changed returns `Done::Load` with the same generation
  (job.rs:118-127).
  - Confirmed: generation identifies one incarnation of an entry and is shared
  by its load, reload, recheck and raster jobs. Only a new entry or a reparse
  bumps it, so results for a removed entry (evict.rs:181) or an older parse are
  dropped. Reload and recheck do not bump it; their states (Reloading, Checking,
  Variant::Pending) prevent a second job from starting.
  - Type: keep it independent of `state` (possibly as a newtype over
  `NonZeroU64`).

          -wn`

          +e key is what matters`                                        
      958 +  - `projection: readonly snapshot of <持ち主の型名>'s state f

  Wrote 1 memory (ctrl+o to expand)

● レビューの26件をすべてコードで確かめたうえで、設計書・作業指示書・計測基盤を更
  新しました（版 1.1）。今回はまだ commit も push もしていません。
  採否の一覧は設計書の 0.4 節に表で入れてあります。

  採用した主な指摘

  - R01（Style の merge が指数的に重くなる）は事実でした。 前回の案は setter
    の呼び出し履歴を記録して再生する方式で、merge
    のたびに再生が倍になります。field
    を8つの群に分け、使った群だけを確保する構造に変えました。履歴は持たず、merge
    の意味は今と同じです。
  - R05（GPU 共有だと GPU がいつまでも起動しない）も事実でした。 GPU 共有は GPU
    が準備できる前には描画内容に現れないため、「描画内容に GPU
    が要る処理が出たら起動」では循環になります。core から明示的に GPU
    の起動を要求する経路（HostRequest::DemandGpu）を足しました。
  - R13 を受けて、pipeline を後から作る案は撤回しました。 初回の操作の途中で
    compile が走り、描画が止まるためです。
  - R07・R20 を受けて #[component] を直しました。
    - 開閉のたびに中身を作り直す content（ViewFn）を props
      として扱えるようにした。
    - 引数の変換をその場で行うので、&str もそのまま渡せる。
  - 付録 A の状態機械を確定させました。
    「確かめて合わなければ止まる」という先送りを無くすため、約30行について全書込
    み箇所と到達する組合せを調べ直し、直した後の型まで決めています。
    - 前回の案は誤りでした。例えば再生と録音は4通りとも有効、VirtualWindow
      の項目は独立、asset の generation は全 job 共通の識別子です。
    - 逆に Step、NodeStyle、SideNavCx、ToolStatus
      などは、実際に起こる組合せだけを表す enum にしました。
  - 完了の条件を明文化しました。 次の4つを満たさない限り、最終段階で完了を宣言せ
    ず、設計者への報告で止まります（作業指示書の最終 wave W7、条件は設計書 1.6
    節）。
    - 正しさ（テストなどの関門）
    - KPI の必達
    - 状態の監査で未解決が 0 件
    - 影響の大きい未実施が無い

    監査を主にし、lint は補助と位置付けています。静的な検査だけでは「全て」を証
    明できないという限界も、設計書に書きました。
  - 計測基盤も作り直しました（R15〜R18）。
    - KPI の全行を出し、失敗したら INVALID として扱う。
    - CPU 時間は小数で記録する。
    - 他の package の feature が混ざらないよう、package を1つずつ build する。

    このマシンで通しの実行も確認しました。ただしその間 OS が表示を約32fps
    に間引いていたので、fps
    と描画間隔は使えません。これを検出して警告を出す処理を足してあります。

  一部だけ採用・維持した点

  - R02（O(changed) になっていない）：
    計算量の説明は実態に合わせて書き直しました。アルゴリズム自体は維持しています
    。変わった親の子を一度ずつ比べる処理は、Taffy がその親を layout
    し直すときの走査と同じ大きさで、安価だからです。
  - R10 の AsyncCell： 二つの signal に分けている既存の決定（ADR-21、通知の細か
    さのため）は保ちました。代わりに、書き込みを publish
    という一つの関数に限定し、そのことを明示しました。
  - crate の分割は増やしていません。 レビューも、今の境界の判断（kivact-macros
    と kivact-bench のみ追加）を妥当としています。

  確認させてください

  更新を commit して push
  してよいですか。別マシンで使うには必要です。レビューのファイル
  20261109_2107_CODEX_REVIEW.md は未追跡のまま残しています。根拠として一緒に
  commit するかも決めてください。

  。HEAD db8d679b である。

  サブエージェントを並列で用いて修正せよ。                                      
                                                                                
  その後、AC-2 から AC-4 の達成に向けて、再度別AIが作業できるように、新しい設計 
  書と作業指示書をリポジトリルートに作成してコミットプッシュせよ。              
  別のAIが別途設計や探索をする必要はなく、ただ単に作業指示書と設計書をもとに淡  
  々と実装できる程度に詳細に記述すること。                                      
  別のAIは賢くない（Haiku 5.5 High程度）。                                      
                                                                                
  実装速度を向上するため、サブエージェントは最大6体を並列稼働させる。サブエージ 
  ェントの割り振りについても指示書に記載せよ。                                  
  また、実装途中に何度も時間のかかるテストを回すことは避けよ。                  
                                                                                
  【実装方針】                                                                  
                                                                                
  - Rust 未経験のReact・SolidJS開発者がスムーズに書ける公開APIになっているか    
  - 公開APIはわかりやすく統一的なAPIであるか                                    
  - 公開APIは普段はJavaScriptやPythonを書いているRust未経験の人でも容易に記述で 
  きるか                                                                        
  - Kivact内部が統一的でシンプルな構造になっているか                            
  - Kivactの長期保守性                                                          
  - docs/for-kivact-developer/strategy.md を簡単に遂行できるか                  
                                                                                
  モジュール分割を行うよりはクレート分割を行うべきだ。ただし、クレート分割は目  
  的ではなく手段であり、境界がはっきりしていないのに分割してはいけない。        
                                                                                
  また、存在する暗黙的な状態マシンは全て解消せよ。
